
===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_foreach_executor.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, func, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, sessionmaker

from app.database import Base
from app.models import WorkflowForeachItem, WorkflowModelInvocation


def _executor_module():
    from app import workflow_foreach_executor

    return workflow_foreach_executor


@pytest.fixture
def session_factory(tmp_path: Path) -> Iterator[sessionmaker[Session]]:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'foreach.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    factory = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        yield factory
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _node(*, max_items: int = 100, failure_policy: str = "fail_open"):
    return {
        "node_instance_id": "foreach_images",
        "kind": "foreach_image",
        "retry_policy": {"max_attempts": 2},
        "config": {
            "items_path": "$.case.images",
            "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
            "max_items": max_items,
            "max_parallel_items": 4,
            "failure_policy": failure_policy,
            "item_executor": {
                "kind": "prompt",
                "prompt_version_id": 17,
                "model_registry_id": 9,
                "thinking_enabled": False,
                "thinking_level": "none",
                "output_contract": {
                    "mode": "json_schema",
                    "schema": {
                        "type": "object",
                        "properties": {"score": {"type": "number"}},
                        "required": ["score"],
                        "additionalProperties": False,
                    },
                },
            },
        },
    }


def _input(*keys: str):
    return {
        "case": {
            "case_id": "case-1",
            "images": [
                {
                    "cloud_id": key,
                    "asset_id": index + 1,
                    "asset_sha256": f"{index + 1:064x}",
                }
                for index, key in enumerate(keys)
            ],
        }
    }


def test_materialization_is_idempotent_and_duplicate_item_keys_fail(
    session_factory,
) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    with session_factory() as db:
        first = foreach.materialize_foreach_items(
            db,
            production_run_id=1,
            parent_step_attempt_id=7,
            node=_node(),
            input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        )
        db.commit()
        second = foreach.materialize_foreach_items(
            db,
            production_run_id=1,
            parent_step_attempt_id=7,
            node=_node(),
            input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        )
        db.commit()

        assert [item.item_key for item in first] == ["a", "b"]
        assert [item.id for item in second] == [item.id for item in first]
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowForeachItem.id))) == 2

        with pytest.raises(foreach.WorkflowForeachError) as raised:
            foreach.materialize_foreach_items(
                db,
                production_run_id=2,
                parent_step_attempt_id=8,
                node=_node(),
                input_manifest=_input("same", "same"),
            )
        assert raised.value.code == "foreach_item_key_duplicate"
        assert db.scalar(
            select(func.count(WorkflowForeachItem.id)).where(
                WorkflowForeachItem.parent_step_attempt_id == 8
            )
        ) == 0


def test_resume_never_reinvokes_succeeded_child(session_factory) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    invocation_keys: list[str] = []

    def invoke(child):
        invocation_keys.append(child.invocation_key)
        return {"score": 0.9 if child.item_key == "a" else 0.8}

    first = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,
        production_run_id=1,
        parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(),
        input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        worker_id="worker-1",
        invoke_item=invoke,
        max_claims=1,
    )
    assert first is None

    output = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,
        production_run_id=1,
        parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(),
        input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        worker_id="worker-2",
        invoke_item=invoke,
    )

    assert invocation_keys == ["item:a:attempt:1", "item:b:attempt:1"]
    assert output is not None
    assert output["counts"] == {
        "total": 2,
        "succeeded": 2,
        "failed": 0,
        "skipped": 0,
    }


def test_parent_heartbeat_runs_before_every_foreach_child(
    session_factory,
) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    heartbeats: list[str] = []

    output = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,
        production_run_id=1,
        parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(),
        input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        worker_id="worker",
        invoke_item=lambda child: {"score": child.ordinal},
        heartbeat_parent=lambda: heartbeats.append("heartbeat"),
    )

    assert output is not None
    assert len(heartbeats) == 2


def test_fail_open_preserves_failure_and_completes_parent(session_factory) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()

    def invoke(child):
        if child.item_key == "b":
            raise foreach.ForeachItemExecutionError(
                "provider_rejected",
                "供应商拒绝该图片",
                retryable=False,
            )
        return {"score": 0.75}

    output = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,
        production_run_id=1,
        parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(failure_policy="fail_open"),
        input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        worker_id="worker",
        invoke_item=invoke,
    )

    assert output is not None
    assert output["schema_version"] == "workflow-foreach-output-v1"
    assert output["counts"] == {
        "total": 2,
        "succeeded": 1,
        "failed": 1,
        "skipped": 0,
    }
    assert [(item["item_key"], item["status"]) for item in output["items"]] == [
        ("a", "succeeded"),
        ("b", "failed"),
    ]
    assert output["items"][1]["error"]["code"] == "provider_rejected"


def test_fail_closed_terminates_after_bounded_retries(session_factory) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    invocation_keys: list[str] = []

    def invoke(child):
        invocation_keys.append(child.invocation_key)
        raise foreach.ForeachItemExecutionError(
            "provider_429",
            "供应商限流",
            retryable=True,
        )

    with pytest.raises(foreach.WorkflowForeachError) as raised:
        foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
            session_factory,
            production_run_id=1,
            parent_step_attempt_id=7,
            node=_node(failure_policy="fail_closed"),
            input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
            worker_id="worker",
            invoke_item=invoke,
        )

    assert raised.value.code == "foreach_child_failed"
    assert invocation_keys == ["item:a:attempt:1", "item:a:attempt:2"]
    assert raised.value.output_manifest["counts"] == {
        "total": 2,
        "succeeded": 0,
        "failed": 1,
        "skipped": 1,
    }

    with session_factory() as db:
        rows = db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowForeachItem).order_by(WorkflowForeachItem.ordinal)
        ).all()
        assert [row.status for row in rows] == ["failed", "skipped"]
        assert rows[0].attempt_count == 2


def test_quota_wait_yields_parent_without_spending_child_attempt(session_factory):
    foreach = _executor_module()
    keys = []
    def invoke(child):
        keys.append(child.invocation_key)
        raise foreach.ForeachItemExecutionError("quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted",
            "local capacity", retryable=True)
    kwargs = dict(production_run_id=1, parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(failure_policy="fail_closed"), input_manifest=_input("a"),
        worker_id="worker", invoke_item=invoke)
    for _ in range(4):
        with pytest.raises(foreach.WorkflowForeachError) as error:
            foreach.execute_foreach_parent(session_factory, **kwargs)
        assert error.value.code == "quota_pool_concurrency_exhausted"
    assert keys == ["item:a:attempt:1"] * 4
    with session_factory() as db:
        row = db.scalar(select(WorkflowForeachItem))
        assert row.status == "retryable"
        assert row.attempt_count == 0


def test_max_items_fails_instead_of_truncating(session_factory) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    dispatches: list[str] = []

    with pytest.raises(foreach.WorkflowForeachError) as raised:
        foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
            session_factory,
            production_run_id=1,
            parent_step_attempt_id=7,
            node=_node(max_items=2),
            input_manifest=_input("a", "b", "c"),
            worker_id="worker",
            invoke_item=lambda child: dispatches.append(child.invocation_key) or {},
        )

    assert raised.value.code == "foreach_max_items_exceeded"
    assert dispatches == []
    with session_factory() as db:
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(WorkflowForeachItem.id))) == 0


def test_new_parent_attempt_inherits_matching_succeeded_child(
    session_factory,
) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    first_calls: list[str] = []
    second_calls: list[str] = []

    assert foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,
        production_run_id=1,
        parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(),
        input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        worker_id="worker-1",
        invoke_item=lambda child: first_calls.append(child.invocation_key)
        or {"score": 1},
        max_claims=1,
    ) is None

    output = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,
        production_run_id=1,
        parent_step_attempt_id=8,
        node=_node(),
        input_manifest=_input("a", "b"),
        worker_id="worker-2",
        invoke_item=lambda child: second_calls.append(child.invocation_key)
        or {"score": 2},
    )

    assert first_calls == ["item:a:attempt:1"]
    assert second_calls == ["item:b:attempt:1"]
    assert output is not None
    assert [json.loads(item.output_manifest_json) for item in _rows(
        session_factory, 8
    )] == [{"score": 1}, {"score": 2}]


@pytest.mark.parametrize('transport', [False, True])
def test_resume_recovers_provider_success_from_the_invocation_ledger(
    session_factory, transport,
) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()
    with session_factory() as db:
        rows = foreach.materialize_foreach_items(
            db,
            production_run_id=1,
            parent_step_attempt_id=7,
            node=_node(),
            input_manifest=_input("a"),
        )
        row = rows[0]
        row.status = "running"
        row.attempt_count = 1
        row.lease_owner = "crashed-worker"
        row.lease_token = "crashed-token"
        row.lease_expires_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(seconds=1)
        db.add(
            WorkflowModelInvocation(
                production_run_id=1,
                step_attempt_id=7,
                invocation_key=(__import__('app.batch_endpoint_recovery', fromlist=['invocation_prefix']).invocation_prefix('item:a:attempt:1')+'online:1' if transport else "item:a:attempt:1"),
                node_instance_id="foreach_images",
                status="succeeded",
                provider="doubao",
                model_id="seed",
                reserved_input_tokens=100,
                reserved_output_tokens=20,
                input_tokens=80,
                output_tokens=10,
                total_tokens=90,
                estimated_cost_micros=12,
                evidence_json=json.dumps(
                    {"result": {"parsed_output": {"score": 0.91}}},
                    separators=(",", ":"),
                ),
            )
        )
        db.commit()

    dispatches: list[str] = []
    output = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory,
        production_run_id=1,
        parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(),
        input_manifest=_input("a"),
        worker_id="recovery-worker",
        invoke_item=lambda child: dispatches.append(child.invocation_key) or {},
    )

    assert dispatches == []
    assert output is not None
    assert output["items"][0]["output"] == {"score": 0.91}


def _rows(session_factory, parent_step_attempt_id: int):
    with session_factory() as db:
        return db.scalars(
            select(WorkflowForeachItem)
            .where(
                WorkflowForeachItem.parent_step_attempt_id
                == parent_step_attempt_id
            )
            .order_by(WorkflowForeachItem.ordinal)
        ).all()


def _lock_timeout():
    from pymysql.err import OperationalError as MysqlError
    from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError

    return OperationalError(
        "UPDATE workflow_foreach_items", {}, MysqlError(1205, "Lock wait timeout")
    )


def test_transient_db_error_during_child_settlement_is_retried(
    session_factory, monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """一次锁等待超时不能把已拿到结果的子项判成「结果未知」终态。"""

    foreach = _executor_module()
    real = foreach._settle_child_success
    calls = {"n": 0}

    def flaky(db, child, output, *, now):
        calls["n"] += 1
        if calls["n"] == 1:
            raise _lock_timeout()
        return real(db, child, output, now=now)

    monkeypatch.setattr(foreach, "_settle_child_success", flaky)
    monkeypatch.setattr(foreach, "_SETTLEMENT_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS", 0, raising=False)
    output = foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
        session_factory, production_run_id=1, parent_step_attempt_id=7,
        node=_node(failure_policy="fail_closed"), input_manifest=_input("a"),
        worker_id="worker", invoke_item=lambda child: {"score": 0.5},
    )
    assert output is not None
    assert output["counts"]["succeeded"] == 1
    (row,) = _rows(session_factory, 7)
    assert row.status == "succeeded" and row.attempt_count == 1


def test_persistent_db_error_during_child_settlement_stays_non_terminal(
    session_factory, monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    foreach = _executor_module()

    def always_locked(*_args, **_kwargs):
        raise _lock_timeout()

    monkeypatch.setattr(foreach, "_settle_child_success", always_locked)
    monkeypatch.setattr(foreach, "_SETTLEMENT_RETRY_DELAY_SECONDS", 0, raising=False)
    with pytest.raises(foreach.WorkflowForeachError) as error:
        foreach.execute_foreach_parent(
            session_factory, production_run_id=1, parent_step_attempt_id=7,
            node=_node(failure_policy="fail_closed"), input_manifest=_input("a"),
            worker_id="worker", invoke_item=lambda child: {"score": 0.5},
        )
    from app.runtime_queue import LOCAL_RUNTIME_WAIT_CODES

    assert error.value.retryable is True
    assert error.value.code in LOCAL_RUNTIME_WAIT_CODES
    (row,) = _rows(session_factory, 7)
    # 留在非终态，由对账（调用账本）或租约到期后修复，不能写成结果未知失败。
    assert row.status == "running"
    assert row.last_error_code != "foreach_item_outcome_unknown"

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_foreach_migration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import create_engine, inspect
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError

from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS


def _legacy_v94_engine(path: Path):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{path}")
    with engine.begin() as connection:
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE baseline_regression_runs (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE production_runs (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE production_step_attempts ("
            "id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, run_id INTEGER NOT NULL)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "CREATE TABLE model_quota_pools (id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            """
            CREATE TABLE workflow_model_invocations (
                id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                baseline_run_id INTEGER
                    REFERENCES baseline_regression_runs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
                production_run_id INTEGER NOT NULL
                    REFERENCES production_runs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
                step_attempt_id INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE
                    REFERENCES production_step_attempts(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
                node_instance_id VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
                status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'reserved',
                provider VARCHAR(40) NOT NULL,
                model_id VARCHAR(160) NOT NULL,
                quota_pool_id INTEGER
                    REFERENCES model_quota_pools(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
                quota_lease_token VARCHAR(80),
                request_correlation_id VARCHAR(200),
                upstream_status_code INTEGER,
                reserved_input_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                reserved_output_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                input_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                output_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                total_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                estimated_cost_micros INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
                error_code VARCHAR(80) NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                error_message TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '',
                evidence_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',
                created_at DATETIME NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
                finished_at DATETIME
            )
            """
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql("INSERT INTO production_runs(id) VALUES (1)")
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO production_step_attempts(id, run_id) VALUES (7, 1)"
        )
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations ("
            "id, production_run_id, step_attempt_id, node_instance_id, status, "
            "provider, model_id, reserved_input_tokens, reserved_output_tokens, "
            "estimated_cost_micros, evidence_json) VALUES "
            "(11, 1, 7, 'prompt', 'succeeded', 'doubao', 'seed-2.0-lite', "
            "1200, 800, 4321, '{\"legacy\":true}')"
        )
    return engine


def test_migration_95_adds_foreach_items_and_multi_invocation_identity(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = _legacy_v94_engine(tmp_path / "workflow-v95.db")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 95)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)

            inspector = inspect(connection)
            assert "workflow_foreach_items" in inspector.get_table_names()
            invocation_columns = {
                column["name"]
                for column in inspector.get_columns("workflow_model_invocations")
            }
            assert "invocation_key" in invocation_columns
            assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT invocation_key, status, estimated_cost_micros, "
                "evidence_json FROM workflow_model_invocations WHERE id=11"
            ).one() == ("root", "succeeded", 4321, '{"legacy":true}')

            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations ("
                "production_run_id, step_attempt_id, invocation_key, "
                "node_instance_id, status, provider, model_id, evidence_json) "
                "VALUES (1, 7, 'item:image-b:attempt:1', 'prompt', "
                "'reserved', 'doubao', 'seed-2.0-lite', '{}')"
            )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO workflow_model_invocations ("
                    "production_run_id, step_attempt_id, invocation_key, "
                    "node_instance_id, status, provider, model_id, evidence_json) "
                    "VALUES (1, 7, 'item:image-b:attempt:1', 'prompt', "
                    "'reserved', 'doubao', 'seed-2.0-lite', '{}')"
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_foreach_item_identity_and_frozen_input_are_database_enforced(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    engine = _legacy_v94_engine(tmp_path / "workflow-v95-items.db")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 95).up(connection)
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO workflow_foreach_items ("
                "production_run_id, parent_step_attempt_id, "
                "parent_node_instance_id, item_key, ordinal, status, "
                "attempt_count, max_attempts, input_manifest_json, input_hash, "
                "output_manifest_json) VALUES "
                "(1, 7, 'foreach_images', 'image-a', 0, 'pending', 0, 2, "
                "'{\"asset_id\":1}', ?, '{}')",
                ("a" * 64,),
            )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO workflow_foreach_items ("
                    "production_run_id, parent_step_attempt_id, "
                    "parent_node_instance_id, item_key, ordinal, status, "
                    "attempt_count, max_attempts, input_manifest_json, input_hash, "
                    "output_manifest_json) VALUES "
                    "(1, 7, 'foreach_images', 'image-a', 1, 'pending', 0, 2, "
                    "'{\"asset_id\":2}', ?, '{}')",
                    ("b" * 64,),
                )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "UPDATE workflow_foreach_items SET item_key='image-renamed' "
                    "WHERE item_key='image-a'"
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_manager_seed.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    CategoryEvaluationV3Revision,
    CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding,
    EvaluationCategoryProfile,
    EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy,
    ModelConfig,
    ModelRegistryEntry,
    PromptVersion,
    WorkflowDefinition,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
    WorkflowVersion,
    WorkflowWorkingCopy,
)
from app.workflow_manager_seed import seed_workflow_manager
from app.workflow_registry import create_workflow_definition, persisted_workflow_manifest
from app.vision_input_policy import SEED_20_PROFILE


CATEGORIES = (
    "space_image",
    "pdf_text",
    "material_image",
    "proposal_text_pdf",
    "model_3d_su",
    "inspiration_image",
)


def _engine(tmp_path, name: str = "workflow-manager-seed.db"):
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / name}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    return engine


def _seed_model(db: Session) -> ModelRegistryEntry:
    source_model = ModelConfig(
        name="存量机制导入模型",
        provider="fixture",
        protocol="custom_json",
        capabilities_json='["text","vision","structured_output"]',
        vision_capability_profile_json=json.dumps(
            dict(SEED_20_PROFILE), separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True
        ),
        description="只用于测试工作流机制导入",
        base_url="https://fixture.invalid",
        api_path="/dry-run",
        model_id="fixture-import-model",
        max_tokens=8192,
        thinking_mode="auto",
        structured_output=True,
        active=True,
    )
    db.add(source_model)
    db.flush()
    model = ModelRegistryEntry(
        role="main",
        name="存量机制导入模型",
        provider="fixture",
        protocol="custom_json",
        capabilities_json='["text","vision","structured_output"]',
        vision_capability_profile_json=json.dumps(
            dict(SEED_20_PROFILE), separators=(",", ":"), sort_keys=True
        ),
        description="只用于测试工作流机制导入",
        base_url="https://fixture.invalid",
        api_path="/dry-run",
        model_id="fixture-import-model",
        max_tokens=8192,
        thinking_mode="high",
        level="standard",
        structured_output=True,
        active=True,
        source_model_config_id=source_model.id,
        created_by="admin",
    )
    db.add(model)
    db.flush()
    return model


def _seed_category(
    db: Session,
    category_key: str,
    *,
    include_prompt_b: bool = True,
    include_rule: bool = True,
) -> EvaluationCategoryProfile:
    prompt_a = PromptVersion(
        category_key=category_key,
        pipeline_scope="shared",
        stage="A",
        name=f"{category_key} 提示词 A",
        version="a-v1",
        system_prompt="A 系统提示词正文",
        user_prompt="A 用户提示词正文",
        rubric_version="rubric-v1",
        status="published",
        created_by="admin",
    )
    db.add(prompt_a)
    db.flush()
    prompt_b = None
    if include_prompt_b:
        prompt_b = PromptVersion(
            category_key=category_key,
            pipeline_scope="shared",
            stage="B",
            name=f"{category_key} 提示词 B",
            version="b-v1",
            system_prompt="B 系统提示词正文",
            user_prompt="B 用户提示词正文",
            rubric_version="rubric-v1",
            status="published",
            created_by="admin",
        )
        db.add(prompt_b)
        db.flush()
    profile = EvaluationCategoryProfile(
        category_key=category_key,
        display_name=f"{category_key} 类目",
        status="active",
        allowed_mime_types_json='["image/jpeg"]',
        preprocess_config_json="{}",
        pipeline_config_json="{}",
        prompt_a_id=prompt_a.id,
        prompt_b_id=prompt_b.id if prompt_b is not None else None,
        rubric_version="rubric-v1",
        created_by="admin",
    )
    db.add(profile)
    if include_rule:
        db.add(
            CategoryEvaluationV3Revision(
                category_key=category_key,
                display_name=f"{category_key} 规则合同",
                revision=1,
                status="active",
                contract_json=json.dumps(
                    {
                        "prompt_bindings": {
                            "call_a_version": "a-v1",
                            "call_b_version": "b-v1",
                        }
                    },
                    ensure_ascii=False,
                ),
                classification_map_json="{}",
                subcategory_dimensions_json="{}",
                dimension_deduction_rules_json="{}",
                media_penalty_enabled=True,
                contract_hash="a" * 64,
                created_by="admin",
            )
        )
    db.flush()
    return profile


def test_knowledge_graph_category_seed_is_independent_and_idempotent(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    from app.knowledge_graph_category_seed import ensure_knowledge_graph_category
    from app.native_workflow_migration import ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow

    engine = _engine(tmp_path, "knowledge-graph-category-seed.db")
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            model = _seed_model(db)
            first_profile = ensure_knowledge_graph_category(
                db,
                actor="system:kg-category-test",
            )
            second_profile = ensure_knowledge_graph_category(
                db,
                actor="system:kg-category-test",
            )
            first_workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                db,
                actor="system:kg-category-test",
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
            )
            second_workflow = ensure_knowledge_graph_workflow(
                db,
                actor="system:kg-category-test",
                category_key="knowledge_graph",
            )
            db.flush()

            assert second_profile.id == first_profile.id
            assert first_profile.category_key == "knowledge_graph"
            assert first_profile.display_name == "知识图谱打标"
            assert first_profile.status == "active"
            assert first_profile.model_config_id == model.source_model_config_id
            assert second_workflow.id == first_workflow.id
            assert first_workflow.version == "kg-native-v2"  # 海外 is_single 1/2 编码后的模板版本
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(first_workflow)
            assert manifest["category_key"] == "knowledge_graph"
            assert manifest["lane_key"] == "knowledge_graph"

            assets = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == "knowledge_graph"
                )
            ).all()
            assert {asset.asset_key for asset in assets} == {
                "kg_domestic_whole_prompt",
                "kg_domestic_single_prompt",
                "kg_overseas_whole_prompt",
                "kg_overseas_single_prompt",
            }
            assert all(asset.kind == "prompt" for asset in assets)
            prompt_versions = db.scalars(
                select(PromptVersion).where(
                    PromptVersion.category_key == "knowledge_graph"
                )
            ).all()
            assert len(prompt_versions) == 4
            assert len({row.id for row in prompt_versions}) == 4
            bindings = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding).join(
                    WorkflowNodeAsset,
                    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == WorkflowNodeAsset.id,
                ).where(WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == "knowledge_graph")
            ).all()
            assert len(bindings) == 4
            assert db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAsset.id).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == "model_3d_su",
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key.like("kg_%"),
                )
            ) is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_seed_imports_all_existing_categories_without_moving_profile_pointers(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            model = _seed_model(db)
            profiles = [_seed_category(db, key) for key in CATEGORIES]
            frozen_pointers = {
                profile.category_key: (profile.prompt_a_id, profile.prompt_b_id)
                for profile in profiles
            }

            seed_workflow_manager(db, actor="system:workflow-manager-import")
            db.flush()

            definitions = db.scalars(select(WorkflowDefinition)).all()
            versions = db.scalars(select(WorkflowVersion)).all()
            assets = db.scalars(select(WorkflowNodeAsset)).all()
            bindings = db.scalars(select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding)).all()
            assert {row.workflow_key for row in definitions} == {
                f"{key}.evaluation" for key in CATEGORIES
            } | {"knowledge_graph.evaluation"}
            assert len(versions) == 13
            assert all(row.status == "active" for row in versions)
            assert len(assets) == 70
            assert len(bindings) == 70
            for version in versions:
                manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(version)
                all_prompt_nodes = [
                    node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
                ]
                assert all(
                    node["config"]["thinking_level"] == "none"
                    and node["config"]["thinking_enabled"] is False
                    for node in all_prompt_nodes
                )
                if version.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3":
                    if manifest.get("lane_key") == "knowledge_graph":
                        assert manifest["category_key"] == "knowledge_graph"
                        assert sum(
                            node["kind"] == "prompt"
                            for node in manifest["nodes"]
                        ) == 4
                        assert sum(
                            node["kind"] == "branch"
                            for node in manifest["nodes"]
                        ) == 1
                        continue
                    assert sum(
                        node["kind"] == "rule_module"
                        for node in manifest["nodes"]
                    ) == 7
                    continue
                prompt_nodes = [
                    node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
                ]
                assert [node["config"]["asset_key"] for node in prompt_nodes] == [
                    "prompt_a",
                    "prompt_b",
                ]
                assert all(
                    node["config"]["model_registry_id"] == model.id
                    and node["config"]["max_tokens"] == 8192
                    for node in prompt_nodes
                )
                assert "incremental" not in manifest["run_modes"]
            assert db.scalar(select(CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding)) is None
            assert {
                profile.category_key: (profile.prompt_a_id, profile.prompt_b_id)
                for profile in db.scalars(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile)).all()
                if profile.category_key in CATEGORIES
            } == frozen_pointers
            knowledge_graph = db.scalar(
                select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
                    EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == "knowledge_graph"
                )
            )
            assert knowledge_graph is not None
            assert (knowledge_graph.prompt_a_id, knowledge_graph.prompt_b_id) == (
                None,
                None,
            )

            counts = (len(definitions), len(versions), len(assets), len(bindings))
            seed_workflow_manager(db, actor="system:workflow-manager-import")
            db.flush()
            assert (
                len(db.scalars(select(WorkflowDefinition)).all()),
                len(db.scalars(select(WorkflowVersion)).all()),
                len(db.scalars(select(WorkflowNodeAsset)).all()),
                len(db.scalars(select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding)).all()),
            ) == counts
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_seed_does_not_overwrite_existing_workflow_and_handles_single_prompt(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path, "workflow-manager-seed-existing.db")
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _seed_model(db)
            legacy_space_profile = _seed_category(db, "space_image")
            legacy_space_profile.prompt_a_id = None
            legacy_space_profile.prompt_b_id = None
            _seed_category(db, "pdf_text", include_prompt_b=False)
            existing = create_workflow_definition(
                db,
                workflow_key="space_image.evaluation",
                name="运营已经维护的工作流",
                description="不能被种子覆盖",
                owner="operations",
                allowed_categories=["space_image"],
                created_by="operator",
            )

            seed_workflow_manager(db, actor="system:workflow-manager-import")
            db.flush()

            assert db.get(WorkflowDefinition, existing.id).name == "运营已经维护的工作流"
            prompt_assets = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowNodeAsset)
                .where(
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == "space_image",
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.kind == "prompt",
                )
                .order_by(WorkflowNodeAsset.asset_key)
            ).all()
            assert [asset.asset_key for asset in prompt_assets] == [
                "prompt_a",
                "prompt_b",
            ]
            for asset, expected_stage in zip(prompt_assets, ("A", "B"), strict=True):
                working_copy = db.scalar(
                    select(EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy).where(
                        EvaluationComponentWorkingCopy.asset_id == asset.id
                    )
                )
                assert working_copy is not None
                assert json.loads(working_copy.content_json) == {
                    "stage": expected_stage,
                    "system_prompt": f"{expected_stage} 系统提示词正文",
                    "user_prompt": f"{expected_stage} 用户提示词正文",
                    "rubric_version": "rubric-v1",
                }
                bound_prompt_ids = db.scalars(
                    select(WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id).where(
                        WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.asset_id == asset.id,
                        WorkflowNodeAssetBinding.prompt_version_id.is_not(None),
                    )
                ).all()
                assert bound_prompt_ids == [working_copy.base_prompt_version_id]
            historical = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowVersion).where(
                    WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == existing.id,
                    WorkflowVersion.version == "legacy-r1",
                )
            )
            assert historical is not None
            assert historical.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3"
            assert db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowWorkingCopy).where(
                    WorkflowWorkingCopy.workflow_definition_id == existing.id
                )
            ) is None
            pdf_definition = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowDefinition).where(
                    WorkflowDefinition.workflow_key == "pdf_text.evaluation"
                )
            )
            pdf_version = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowVersion).where(
                    WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id == pdf_definition.id,
                    WorkflowVersion.version == "imported-1",
                )
            )
            manifest = persisted_workflow_manifest(pdf_version)
            assert [
                node["config"]["asset_key"]
                for node in manifest["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            ] == ["prompt_a"]
            prompt_policy = next(
                node["config"]["vision_input_policy"]
                for node in manifest["nodes"]
                if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            assert prompt_policy["roles"]["target"] == {
                "fidelity": {"mode": "detail", "value": "high"},
                "preprocess": {"mode": "original"},
            }
            assert prompt_policy["roles"]["anchor"] == {
                "fidelity": {"mode": "pixel_limit", "min_pixels": 196 * 196, "max_pixels": 1024 * 1024},
                "preprocess": {"mode": "short_edge", "pixels": 1024},
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

                select(WorkflowDefinition).where(

                "dimension_scoring_5",
                "anchor_calibration_6",

                "downstream",

            from app.workflow_v3 import validate_managed_workflow_v3

            assert validate_managed_workflow_v3(db, template.manifest) == []
            assert db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowWorkingCopy).where(
                    WorkflowWorkingCopy.workflow_definition_id == definition.id
                )
            ) is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_seed_marks_missing_required_configuration_as_blocked(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = _engine(tmp_path, "workflow-manager-seed-blocked.db")
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _seed_category(db, "material_image", include_rule=False)

            seed_workflow_manager(db, actor="system:workflow-manager-import")
            db.flush()

            version = db.scalar(select(WorkflowVersion))
            assert version is not None
            assert version.status == "blocked"
            report = json.loads(version.validation_report_json)
            assert report["ok"] is False
            assert {item["code"] for item in report["errors"]} == {
                "workflow_import_model_missing",
                "workflow_import_rule_missing",
            }
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_model_daily_budget.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from threading import Barrier

import pytest
from pathlib import Path

_BACKEND_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
from sqlalchemy import inspect, select
from sqlalchemy.dialects import mysql, postgresql
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session
from sqlalchemy.schema import CreateTable

from app.migrations.mysql_baseline import (
    create_mysql_baseline,
    mysql_metadata,
    upgrade_mysql_schema,
)
from app.migrations.postgres_baseline import postgres_metadata
from app.migrations.runner import MIGRATIONS
from app.models import WorkflowModelDailyBudget


EXPECTED_COLUMNS = {
    "id",
    "business_date_utc",
    "scope",
    "request_count",
    "reserved_input_tokens",
    "reserved_output_tokens",
    "estimated_cost_micros",
    "created_at",
    "updated_at",
}


def test_incremental_daily_budget_environment_can_only_lower_hard_caps(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    monkeypatch.setenv("TEST_INCREMENTAL_DAILY_CAP", "1001")
    assert workflow_model_budget._env_budget_at_most(
        "TEST_INCREMENTAL_DAILY_CAP",
        1000,
    ) == 1000

    monkeypatch.setenv("TEST_INCREMENTAL_DAILY_CAP", "999")
    assert workflow_model_budget._env_budget_at_most(
        "TEST_INCREMENTAL_DAILY_CAP",
        1000,
    ) == 999


def test_migration_91_creates_idempotent_daily_budget_ledger(tmp_path) -> None:
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'daily-budget-v91.db'}")
    try:
        with engine.begin() as connection:
            migration = next(item for item in MIGRATIONS if item.version == 91)
            migration.up(connection)
            migration.up(connection)
        inspector = inspect(engine)
        assert EXPECTED_COLUMNS == {
            item["name"]
            for item in inspector.get_columns("workflow_model_daily_budgets")
        }
        assert {
            tuple(item["column_names"])
            for item in inspector.get_unique_constraints(
                "workflow_model_daily_budgets"
            )
        } == {("business_date_utc", "scope")}
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_mysql_and_postgres_baselines_compile_daily_budget_contract() -> None:
    for metadata, dialect in (
        (mysql_metadata(), mysql.dialect()),
        (postgres_metadata(), postgresql.dialect()),
    ):
        table = metadata.tables["workflow_model_daily_budgets"]
        assert EXPECTED_COLUMNS == set(table.c.keys())
        ddl = str(CreateTable(table).compile(dialect=dialect)).lower()
        assert "unique" in ddl
        assert "business_date_utc" in ddl
        for column in (
            "request_count",
            "reserved_input_tokens",
            "reserved_output_tokens",
            "estimated_cost_micros",
        ):
            assert str(table.c[column].type.compile(dialect=dialect)).lower() == (
                "bigint"
            )


def test_mysql_v90_upgrades_additively_to_v91(mysql_engine) -> None:
    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection, install_invariants=False)
        connection.exec_driver_sql("DROP TABLE workflow_model_daily_budgets")
        connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "DELETE FROM schema_migrations WHERE version >= 91"
        )
        upgrade_mysql_schema(connection, expected=91)

        assert "workflow_model_daily_budgets" in inspect(
            connection
        ).get_table_names()
        assert connection.exec_driver_sql(
            "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
        ).scalar_one() == 91


def test_mysql_daily_budget_reservation_is_atomic(
    mysql_engine,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
) -> None:
    """门槛开着时，两个并发预留只能有一个成功——这测的是加锁，不是门槛本身。"""

    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    with mysql_engine.begin() as connection:
        create_mysql_baseline(connection)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        workflow_model_budget, "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        workflow_model_budget,
        "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_REQUESTS",
        1,
    )
    start = Barrier(2)

    def reserve_once() -> str:
        with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
            start.wait(timeout=5)
            try:
                workflow_model_budget._enforce_incremental_daily_budget(
                    db,
                    reserved_input=100,
                    reserved_output=10,
                    estimated_cost=1_000,
                )
                db.commit()
                return "reserved"
            except workflow_model_budget.WorkflowModelBudgetError as exc:
                db.rollback()
                return exc.code

    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=2) as executor:
        outcomes = sorted(executor.map(lambda _index: reserve_once(), range(2)))

    assert outcomes == ["incremental_daily_requests_exhausted", "reserved"]
    with Session(mysql_engine) as db:
        ledger = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
        assert ledger is not None
        assert ledger.scope == "incremental_real"
        assert ledger.request_count == 1
        assert ledger.reserved_input_tokens == 100
        assert ledger.reserved_output_tokens == 10
        assert ledger.estimated_cost_micros == 1_000


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 2026-09-16 老大裁决：取消这道日门槛。
#
# 它挡掉的不是误操作，是正常运行：上限 1000 元/天是硬编码的，而同一份注释写明的
# 目标吞吐每天要 ~6800 元，于是链路每天跑到 15% 左右就停死，停下来的活既不会自己
# 恢复（要等 UTC 翻天）也没人去处理。账本保留，拒绝取消。
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def _ledger_state(db):
    ledger = db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
    assert ledger is not None
    return (ledger.request_count, ledger.reserved_input_tokens,
            ledger.reserved_output_tokens, ledger.estimated_cost_micros)


@pytest.fixture
def sqlite_budget_db(tmp_path):
    from sqlalchemy import create_engine

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'budget.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    with Session(engine) as db:
        yield db
    engine.dispose()


def test_the_daily_ceiling_no_longer_refuses_a_request(sqlite_budget_db, monkeypatch):
    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    monkeypatch.setattr(workflow_model_budget, "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_REQUESTS", 1)
    monkeypatch.setattr(workflow_model_budget, "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_COST_MICROS", 1)
    for _ in range(3):
        workflow_model_budget._enforce_incremental_daily_budget(
            sqlite_budget_db, reserved_input=100, reserved_output=10, estimated_cost=1_000)
    sqlite_budget_db.commit()
    # 远远超过上限，但一次都没拒。
    assert _ledger_state(sqlite_budget_db) == (3, 300, 30, 3_000)


def test_the_ledger_still_records_every_call(sqlite_budget_db, monkeypatch):
    """去掉的是「到点就拒」，不是记账——花了多少仍然要看得见。"""

    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    workflow_model_budget._enforce_incremental_daily_budget(
        sqlite_budget_db, reserved_input=1_500, reserved_output=140, estimated_cost=34_000)
    workflow_model_budget._enforce_incremental_daily_budget(
        sqlite_budget_db, reserved_input=1_500, reserved_output=140, estimated_cost=34_000)
    sqlite_budget_db.commit()
    assert _ledger_state(sqlite_budget_db) == (2, 3_000, 280, 68_000)
    ledger = sqlite_budget_db.scalar(select(WorkflowModelDailyBudget))
    assert ledger.scope == "incremental_real"


def _flag_in_fresh_process(name: str, value: str | None) -> bool:
    """Import the budget module in a child process with one env var set.

    2026-09-17: reloading ``app.workflow_model_budget`` in-process rebinds
    ``WorkflowModelBudgetError`` and every function to new objects, while the
    executor and other test modules keep the originals. Any test that then
    reserves a call gets ``model_execution_preflight_failed`` instead of the
    budget error it expects: 11 failures in the release order on 2026-09-17,
    none of them real. Read the env-derived flag where it is actually decided,
    at interpreter start, and never reload the module in the test process.
    """
    import os
    import subprocess
    import sys

    env = {key: item for key, item in os.environ.items() if key != name}
    if value is not None:
        env[name] = value
    code = f"import app.workflow_model_budget as m; print(m.{name})"
    result = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", code], env=env, capture_output=True, text=True,
                            cwd=str(_BACKEND_ROOT), check=True)
    return {"True": True, "False": False}[result.stdout.strip()]


def test_it_is_off_by_default_and_can_be_switched_back_on_without_a_release():
    assert _flag_in_fresh_process("INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", None) is False
    for value in ("1", "true", "TRUE", "yes", "on"):
        assert _flag_in_fresh_process("INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", value) is True, value
    for value in ("", "0", "false", "no", "off", "maybe"):
        assert _flag_in_fresh_process("INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", value) is False, value


def test_switching_it_back_on_restores_the_refusal(sqlite_budget_db, monkeypatch):
    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    monkeypatch.setattr(workflow_model_budget, "INCREMENTAL_DAILY_BUDGET_ENFORCED", True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(workflow_model_budget, "MAX_INCREMENTAL_REAL_DAILY_REQUESTS", 1)
    workflow_model_budget._enforce_incremental_daily_budget(
        sqlite_budget_db, reserved_input=1, reserved_output=1, estimated_cost=1)
    with pytest.raises(workflow_model_budget.WorkflowModelBudgetError) as info:
        workflow_model_budget._enforce_incremental_daily_budget(
            sqlite_budget_db, reserved_input=1, reserved_output=1, estimated_cost=1)
    assert info.value.code == "incremental_daily_requests_exhausted"


def test_the_per_run_regression_caps_are_untouched():
    """只取消了跨运行的日门槛；单轮存量回归自己的上限不在这次范围内。"""

    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    assert workflow_model_budget.MAX_REAL_REQUESTS == 10_000
    assert workflow_model_budget.MAX_REAL_COST_MICROS == 1_000_000_000
    # 这些错误码仍然可能出现，执行器对它们的处理不能一起删掉。
    from app.batch_endpoint_recovery import BUDGET_EXHAUSTED_CODES
    assert "model_budget_cost_exhausted" in BUDGET_EXHAUSTED_CODES


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# 同一次裁决的另一半：单轮存量回归的全局天花板 MAX_REAL_* 也取消。
# 注意它和「本轮自己的冻结预算」是两回事——后者没关，也不该关。
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #


def test_the_global_real_ceilings_are_off_by_default():
    assert _flag_in_fresh_process("REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED", None) is False
    assert _flag_in_fresh_process("REAL_EXECUTION_CEILINGS_ENFORCED", "1") is True


# 「超过全局天花板的一轮仍然建得起来」由 test_native_workflow_real_regression.py 的
# test_budget_preview_reports_cost_and_does_not_block_when_over_cap 覆盖——那边有
# _priced_model/_prompt 这些能造出合法提示词节点的助手，在这里重搭一份只会搭歪。


def test_the_per_run_frozen_budget_is_still_enforced():
    """关掉的只是全局天花板。每一轮仍然被自己冻结的那份预算约束——
    没有它，一轮跑飞就没有任何东西拦得住。"""

    import inspect
    import app.workflow_model_budget as workflow_model_budget

    source = inspect.getsource(workflow_model_budget.reserve_model_invocation)
    assert '_positive_budget_value(budget, key)' in source
    assert 'if projected > limit:' in source
    # 纠偏重跑那条路上 MAX_REAL_* 本身就是预算，不是天花板，所以保留。
    assert 'if paid_correction_checkpoint is not None:' in source

def _sha256(value: bytes | str) -> str:
    raw = value if isinstance(value, bytes) else value.encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "provider",
    ["ark", "doubao", "volcengine", "volcengine-ark"],
)
def test_prompt_thinking_defaults_off_until_operator_explicitly_enables_it(
    provider: str,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_executor import _runtime_thinking_controls

    assert _runtime_thinking_controls(
        provider=provider,
        default_thinking_mode="auto",
        thinking_level="high",
        thinking_enabled=False,
    ) == ("disabled", None)
    assert _runtime_thinking_controls(
        provider=provider,
        default_thinking_mode="auto",
        thinking_level="none",
        thinking_enabled=False,
    ) == ("disabled", None)
    assert _runtime_thinking_controls(
        provider=provider,
        default_thinking_mode="auto",
        thinking_level="high",
        thinking_enabled=True,
    ) == ("enabled", "high")


def _case(
    db: Session,
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
    *,
    knowledge_graph: bool = False,
    strict_output: bool = False,
    explicit_vision: bool = False,
    vision_profile: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> tuple[ProductionRun, ProductionStepAttempt, dict[str, Any], ModelConfig, PromptVersion]:
    # 必须是真实可解码的图片：执行器现在与老评测路径共用同一套输入预处理
    # （压缩超大图、动图取帧），伪造字节会被 Image.open 当场拒绝。
    import io

    from PIL import Image

    buffer = io.BytesIO()
    Image.new("RGB", (640, 480), (12, 34, 56)).save(buffer, format="JPEG")
    raw_image = buffer.getvalue()
    image_path = tmp_path / "workflow-model.jpg"
    image_path.write_bytes(raw_image)
    asset = Asset(
        original_name="workflow-model.jpg",
        stored_name=image_path.name,
        mime_type="image/jpeg",
        size_bytes=len(raw_image),
        width=640,
        height=480,
        sha256=_sha256(raw_image),
        category_key="model_3d_su" if knowledge_graph else "space_image",
    )
    frozen_vision_profile = dict(vision_profile or SEED_20_PROFILE)
    model = ModelConfig(
        name="冻结执行模型",
        provider="doubao",
        protocol="openai_chat",
        base_url="https://fixture.invalid",
        api_path="/chat/completions",
        model_id="doubao-frozen-model",
        capabilities_json='["text","vision","structured_output"]',
        vision_capability_profile_json=canonical_json(frozen_vision_profile),
        encrypted_api_key="encrypted:test-only-reference",
        temperature=0.1,
        max_tokens=4_096,
        max_input_tokens=12_000,
        timeout_seconds=120,
        max_retries=2,
        structured_output=True,
        thinking_mode="auto",
        input_micros_per_million_tokens=1_000_000,
        output_micros_per_million_tokens=2_000_000,
        benchmark_enabled=True,
        active=True,
    )
    prompt = PromptVersion(
        category_key=asset.category_key,
        pipeline_scope="shared",
        stage="KG_DW" if knowledge_graph else "A",
        name="冻结提示词",
        version="prompt-v1",
        system_prompt=(
            "冻结系统提示词，素材标识 {{ASSET_ID}}"
            if knowledge_graph
            else "冻结系统提示词"
        ),
        user_prompt=(
            "请按冻结字段合同返回结果，主分类 {{PRIMARY_CATEGORY}}"
            if knowledge_graph
            else (
                "元数据 {{image_metadata}}\n"
                "上游 {{precheck_json}}\n"
                "前一步 {{previous_output}}\n"
                "口径 {{rubric_version}}"
            )
        ),
        rubric_version="rubric-v7",
        status="published",
        source="workflow_component",
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add_all([asset, model, prompt])
    db.flush()

    component_content = {
        "stage": prompt.stage,
        "system_prompt": prompt.system_prompt,
        "user_prompt": prompt.user_prompt,
        "rubric_version": prompt.rubric_version,
    }
    model_snapshot = {
        "model_registry_id": 7,
        "name": model.name,
        "provider": model.provider,
        "protocol": model.protocol,
        "model_id": model.model_id,
        "source_model_config_id": model.id,
        "capabilities": ["text", "vision", "structured_output"],
        "structured_output": True,
        "runtime_config": build_model_config_snapshot(model),
    }
    model_snapshot["snapshot_hash"] = _sha256(canonical_json(model_snapshot))
    node: dict[str, Any] = {
        "node_instance_id": "prompt_frozen",
        "kind": "prompt",
        "display_name": "冻结提示词节点",
        "config": {
            "asset_key": "prompt_frozen",
            "prompt_version_id": prompt.id,
            "model_registry_id": 7,
            "max_tokens": 512,
            "thinking_level": "high",
            "thinking_enabled": True,
            "temperature": 0.25,
            "structured_output": True,
            "component_snapshot": {
                "kind": "prompt",
                "version_id": prompt.id,
                "version": prompt.version,
                "content": component_content,
                "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(component_content),
            },
            "model_snapshot": model_snapshot,
        },
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "score": {"type": "number"},
                "label": {"type": "string"},
            },
            "required": ["score", "label"],
            "additionalProperties": False,
        },
        "error_policy": {"kind": "fail"},
        "retry_policy": {"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 1},
        "evidence_fields": [],
    }
    if strict_output:
        node["config"]["output_contract"] = {
            "mode": "json_schema",
            "name": "workflow_result",
            "description": "冻结提示词输出",
            "strict": True,
            "schema": deepcopy(node["output_schema"]),
        }
    if explicit_vision:
        capability = parse_vision_capability_profile(
            frozen_vision_profile,
            provider=model.provider,
            model_id=model.model_id,
        )
        policy_value = default_vision_input_policy(capability).as_dict()
        policy_value["roles"]["anchor"]["fidelity"] = {
            "mode": "detail",
            "value": "low",
        }
        policy = parse_vision_input_policy(
            policy_value,
            capability=capability,
            legacy_if_missing=False,
        )
        node["config"].update(
            {
                "vision_input_policy": policy.as_dict(),
                "vision_capability_snapshot": capability.as_dict(),
                "vision_policy_hash": vision_policy_hash(policy, capability),
            }
        )
    frozen_input: dict[str, Any] = {
        "content_key": f"asset:{asset.id}",
        "asset_id": asset.id,
        "asset_sha256": asset.sha256,
        "source": "domestic",
        "is_single": 0,
        "ll_id": "ll-frozen-1",
        "authorization": "must-not-reach-model-context",
        "expected_result": {
            "score": 100,
            "leak_marker": "truth-must-not-reach-model",
        },
        "workflow_fixture": {
            "schema_version": "native-prompt-fixtures-v1",
            "prompt_outputs": {
                "prompt_frozen": {
                    "leak_marker": "fixture-must-not-reach-model"
                }
            },
        },
        "fixture_key": "simulation-route-must-not-reach-model",
    }
    lane_key = "stock_regression"
    if knowledge_graph:
        from app.knowledge_graph_contracts import get_knowledge_graph_contract

        contract = get_knowledge_graph_contract(
            "kg.model_3d_su.domestic.whole.v1"
        )
        assert contract is not None
        node["config"]["output_contract_key"] = contract.contract_key
        node["config"]["output_contract_snapshot"] = contract.snapshot()
        node["output_schema"] = contract.output_schema
        lane_key = "knowledge_graph"

    manifest = {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": asset.category_key,
        "lane_key": lane_key,
        "run_modes": ["stock_regression"],
        "nodes": [node],
        "edges": [],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {},
        "layout": {},
    }
    definition = WorkflowDefinition(
        workflow_key=f"model-executor-{int(knowledge_graph)}",
        name="模型执行器测试",
        description="",
        owner="platform",
        allowed_categories_json=canonical_json([asset.category_key]),
        status="active",
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    workflow = WorkflowVersion(
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="1",
        status="active",
        workflow_schema_version="workflow-v3",
        step_manifest_json=canonical_json([node]),
        edge_manifest_json="[]",
        input_schema_json=canonical_json(manifest["input_schema"]),
        output_schema_json=canonical_json(manifest["output_schema"]),
        resource_policy_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "category_key": asset.category_key,
                "lane_key": lane_key,
                "run_modes": ["stock_regression"],
                "queue_class": "validation",
                "layout": {},
            }
        ),
        canonical_hash=_sha256(canonical_json(manifest)),
        validation_report_json=canonical_json({"ok": True, "errors": []}),
        created_by="ops",
    )
    db.add(workflow)
    script = ensure_composer_fixture_version(db, "prompt")
    db.flush()

    baseline = BaselineSet(
        name=f"executor-budget-{workflow.id}", category_key=asset.category_key,
        default_expected_level="L1", fingerprint="b" * 64,
    )
    db.add(baseline)
    db.flush()
    aggregate = BaselineRegressionRun(
        baseline_set_id=baseline.id, sequence_no=1, workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
        execution_engine="native_workflow", strategy_snapshot_json="{}",
        baseline_set_fingerprint=baseline.fingerprint, total=1,
    )
    db.add(aggregate)
    db.flush()
    runtime_context = {
        "run_mode": "stock_regression",
        "baseline_run_id": aggregate.id,
        "baseline_item_id": 1,
        "real_execution_confirmed": True,
        "real_execution_budget": {
            "max_requests": 2,
            "max_input_tokens": 18_000,
            "max_output_tokens": 768,
            "max_cost_micros": 50_000,
        },
        "real_execution_node_limits": {
            "prompt_frozen": {
                "provider": model.provider,
                "model_id": model.model_id,
                "source_model_config_id": model.id,
                "max_input_tokens": model.max_input_tokens,
                "max_output_tokens": 512,
                "estimated_cost_micros": 13_024,
                "input_micros_per_million_tokens": 1_000_000,
                "output_micros_per_million_tokens": 2_000_000,
            }
        },
        "environment": "dry_run",
        "model_execution_mode": "real",
        "category_key": asset.category_key,
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_manifest": frozen_input,
    }
    snapshot = {
        "schema_version": "production-run-snapshot-v1",
        "workflow": {
            "workflow_definition_id": definition.id,
            "workflow_version_id": workflow.id,
            "version": workflow.version,
            "canonical_hash": workflow.canonical_hash,
            "manifest": manifest,
        },
        "scripts": [],
        "runtime_context": runtime_context,
    }
    encoded_snapshot = canonical_json(snapshot)
    run = ProductionRun(
        run_key=f"model-executor-run-{int(knowledge_graph)}",
        idempotency_key=f"model-executor-idempotency-{int(knowledge_graph)}",
        source_type="baseline_regression_item",
        source_id=1,
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
        snapshot_json=encoded_snapshot,
        snapshot_hash=_sha256(encoded_snapshot),
        category_key=asset.category_key,
        queue_class="validation",
        status="running",
        current_step_key="prompt_frozen",
        blockers_json="[]",
        requested_by="ops",
        owner="baseline-regression",
        reason="真实模型执行器测试",
        environment="dry_run",
        model_execution_mode="real",
        total_steps=1,
        completed_steps=0,
        failed_steps=0,
        attempt_count=1,
    )
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    input_manifest = {
        "upstream": {
            "upstream_1": {
                "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                "output": {
                    "score": 42,
                    "password": "must-not-reach-model-context",
                },
            }
        }
    }
    leased_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    lease_expires_at = leased_at + timedelta(minutes=5)
    run.lease_owner = "worker-1"
    run.lease_token = "lease-safe"
    run.lease_expires_at = lease_expires_at
    run.heartbeat_at = leased_at
    attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
        run_id=run.id,
        step_key="prompt_frozen",
        step_type="model_call",
        sequence=0,
        script_version_id=script.id,
        status="running",
        attempt_no=1,
        idempotency_key=f"{run.run_key}:prompt_frozen:attempt:1",
        input_manifest_json=canonical_json(input_manifest),
        input_hash=_sha256(canonical_json(input_manifest)),
        output_manifest_json="{}",
        checkpoint_json="{}",
        lease_owner="worker-1",
        lease_token="lease-safe",
        lease_expires_at=lease_expires_at,
        heartbeat_at=leased_at,
    )
    db.add(attempt)
    db.flush()
    db.add(
        RuntimeDispatchItem(
            step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
            queue_class="validation",
            priority=50,
            status="leased",
            attempt_count=1,
            max_attempts=script.max_attempts,
            lease_owner="worker-1",
            lease_token="lease-safe",
            leased_at=leased_at,
            heartbeat_at=leased_at,

    db.flush()

    import app.workflow_model_executor as executor

    monkeypatch.setattr(
