    return run, attempt, node, model, prompt


def _forbidden_key(value: Any) -> str | None:
    forbidden = {
        "api_key",
        "authorization",
        "cookie",
        "dsn",
        "password",
        "secret",
        "token",
    }
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        for key, child in value.items():
            if str(key).lower() in forbidden:
                return str(key)
            found = _forbidden_key(child)
            if found is not None:
                return found
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for child in value:
            found = _forbidden_key(child)
            if found is not None:
                return found
    return None


def _foreach_snapshot_node(node: Mapping[str, Any], *, max_items: int = 3):
    outer = deepcopy(dict(node))
    outer["kind"] = "foreach_image"
    item_executor = deepcopy(dict(node["config"]))
    item_executor["kind"] = "prompt"
    outer["config"] = {
        "items_path": "$.case.images",
        "item_identity_path": "$.cloud_id",
        "max_items": max_items,
        "max_parallel_items": 2,
        "failure_policy": "fail_open",
        "item_executor": item_executor,
    }
    outer["output_schema"] = {"type": "object"}
    return outer


def test_foreach_child_uses_derived_frozen_prompt_and_explicit_call_identity(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation
    from app.workflow_model_executor import prepare_prompt_invocation
    from app.workflow_prompt_execution import prompt_execution_node

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'foreach-model.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            outer = _foreach_snapshot_node(node)
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] = [outer]
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            frozen_run_input = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
            # Match one materialized design-case foreach child: the legacy
            # identifiers remain available for downstream correlation, while
            # the platform identity and digest authorize the real image read.
            child_input = {
                "cloud_id": "image-a",
                "oss_url": "https://example.invalid/image-a.jpg",
                "asset_id": frozen_run_input["asset_id"],
                "asset_sha256": frozen_run_input["asset_sha256"],
            }
            child_hash = _sha256(canonical_json(child_input))
            executable = prompt_execution_node(outer)
            assert executable is not None

            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=executable,
                input_manifest=child_input,
                invocation_key="item:image-a:attempt:1",
                frozen_input_override=child_input,
                input_fingerprint=child_hash,
                invocation_attempt_no=1,
            )
            assert prepared.invocation_key == "item:image-a:attempt:1"
            assert prepared.input_fingerprint == child_hash
            assert prepared.node["kind"] == "prompt"

            invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=attempt.id,
                node=executable,
                run_snapshot=snapshot,
                invocation_key=prepared.invocation_key,
                reserved_images=len(prepared.media_dispatch_identities),
                dispatch_identity=prepared.dispatch_identity,
            )
            replay = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=attempt.id,
                node=executable,
                run_snapshot=snapshot,
                invocation_key=prepared.invocation_key,
                reserved_images=len(prepared.media_dispatch_identities),
                dispatch_identity=prepared.dispatch_identity,
            )
            assert replay.id == invocation.id
            assert invocation.invocation_key == "item:image-a:attempt:1"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_foreach_budget_counts_the_frozen_child_ceiling(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_budget import (
        real_execution_budget_for_selection,
        real_execution_node_limits,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'foreach-budget.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            _run, _attempt, node, model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            outer = _foreach_snapshot_node(node, max_items=3)
            manifest = {"nodes": [outer], "edges": []}
            limits = real_execution_node_limits(
                manifest,
                model_configs={model.id: model},
            )
            budget = real_execution_budget_for_selection(
                manifest,
                2,
                model_configs={model.id: model},
            )

            assert set(limits) == {"prompt_frozen"}
            assert budget.expected_requests == 6
            assert budget.expected_input_tokens == model.max_input_tokens * 6
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_prompt_executor_uses_only_frozen_prompt_model_and_safe_context(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Catches mutable prompt/model state or secrets changing a paid request."""

    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'model-executor.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            prompt.system_prompt = "被修改后的当前提示词"
            prompt.user_prompt = "被修改后的当前正文"
            db.flush()
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )

            assert prepared.system_prompt == "冻结系统提示词"
            assert "被修改" not in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "{{" not in prepared.user_prompt
            assert '"width":640' in prepared.user_prompt
            assert '"score":42' in prepared.user_prompt
            assert '上游 {"score":42}' in prepared.user_prompt
            assert '前一步 {"score":42}' in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "rubric-v7" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "must-not-reach-model-context" not in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "truth-must-not-reach-model" not in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "fixture-must-not-reach-model" not in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "simulation-route-must-not-reach-model" not in (
                prepared.user_prompt
            )
            assert prepared.image_path == (tmp_path / "workflow-model.jpg")
            assert prepared.mime_type == "image/jpeg"
            assert prepared.media_bundle.target.asset_id is not None
            assert prepared.media_dispatch_identities == ("target:0",)

            calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

            class FakeDoubaoClient:
                def __init__(self, config):
                    calls.append({"config": config})

                async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                    calls[-1].update(
                        {
                            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
                            "user_prompt": user_prompt,
                            "options": options,
                        }
                    )
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 88.5, "label": "好"},
                        raw_text='{"score":88.5,"label":"好"}',
                        raw_payload={
                            "id": "req-safe-1",
                            "authorization": "must-not-persist",
                            "nested": {"token": "must-not-persist"},
                        },
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-safe-1",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_tokens=123,
                        output_tokens=45,
                        total_tokens=168,
                    )

            result = asyncio.run(
                execute_prepared_prompt(
                    prepared,
                    client_type=FakeDoubaoClient,
                )
            )

            assert len(calls) == 1
            config = calls[0]["config"]
            assert config.encrypted_api_key == "encrypted:test-only-reference"
            assert config.provider == "doubao"
            assert config.model_id == "doubao-frozen-model"
            assert config.temperature == 0.25
            assert config.max_tokens == 512
            assert config.max_retries == 0
            assert config.thinking_mode == "enabled"
            assert config.reasoning_effort == "high"
            assert calls[0]["system_prompt"] == "冻结系统提示词"
            assert calls[0]["options"] == {
                "image_path": tmp_path / "workflow-model.jpg",
                "mime_type": "image/jpeg",
                "max_attempts": 1,
                "output_budget": 512,
                "reasoning_effort": "high",
                "structured_output": True,
            }
            assert result.node_result["output"] == {
                "score": 88.5,
                "label": "好",
            }
            assert result.input_tokens == 123
            assert result.output_tokens == 45
            assert result.invocation_evidence["request_correlation_id"] == (
                "req-safe-1"
            )
            assert result.invocation_evidence["component_hash"] == (
                node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content_hash"]
            )
            assert _forbidden_key(result.node_result) is None
            assert "must-not-persist" not in canonical_json(result.node_result)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_explicit_vision_policy_builds_provider_samples_and_official_media_row(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app.doubao import ProviderImageSample
    from app.model_media_usage import record_prepared_media
    from app.model_pricing import doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing, price_usage
    from app.workflow_model_budget import reserve_model_invocation
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        _dispatch_identity,
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'explicit-vision.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db,
                tmp_path,
                monkeypatch,
                explicit_vision=True,
            )
            pricing = doubao_seed_2_0_lite_pricing()
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            limits = snapshot["runtime_context"]["real_execution_node_limits"][
                "prompt_frozen"
            ]
            limits["pricing_snapshot"] = pricing
            limits["estimated_cost_micros"] = price_usage(
                pricing,
                input_tokens=limits["max_input_tokens"],
                output_tokens=limits["max_output_tokens"],
            ).total_cost_micros
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            input_manifest = json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
            )

            assert prepared.image_samples is not None
            assert all(
                isinstance(sample, ProviderImageSample)
                for sample in prepared.image_samples
            )
            assert [sample.role for sample in prepared.image_samples] == ["target"]
            assert prepared.image_samples[0].fidelity.value == "high"
            assert prepared.serialized_provider_request is not None
            assert prepared.dispatch_identity
            assert prepared.media_pricing_snapshot == {
                "version": "model-pricing-v3",
                "pricing_contract": pricing,
            }
            replayed = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=input_manifest,
            )
            assert replayed.dispatch_identity == prepared.dispatch_identity
            assert [
                (sample.role, sample.ordinal)
                for sample in replayed.image_samples or ()
            ] == [("target", 0)]
            assert _dispatch_identity(
                node=prepared.node,
                input_fingerprint=prepared.input_fingerprint,
                media_bundle=prepared.media_bundle,
                media_dispatch_identities=prepared.media_dispatch_identities,
                policy_hash="f" * 64,
                serialized_request=prepared.serialized_provider_request,
            ) != prepared.dispatch_identity

            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            invocation = reserve_model_invocation(
                db,
                attempt_id=attempt.id,
                node=node,
                run_snapshot=snapshot,
                reserved_images=1,
                dispatch_identity=prepared.dispatch_identity,
            )
            rows = record_prepared_media(db, invocation.id, prepared.media_bundle)
            assert [(row.media_role, row.ordinal) for row in rows] == [
                ("target", 0)
            ]
            assert rows[0].image_tokens == 1280
            assert rows[0].usage_source == "official_estimate"

            captured: dict[str, Any] = {}

            class FakeClient:
                def __init__(self, _config):
                    pass

                async def chat_json_images(self, _system, samples, **options):
                    captured["samples"] = tuple(samples)
                    captured["options"] = options
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 88.5, "label": "好"},
                        raw_text='{"score":88.5,"label":"好"}',
                        raw_payload={},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-explicit-vision",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_tokens=10,
                        output_tokens=5,
                        total_tokens=15,
                    )

            asyncio.run(execute_prepared_prompt(prepared, client_type=FakeClient))

            assert captured["samples"] == prepared.image_samples
            assert captured["options"]["prepared_request"] == (
                prepared.serialized_provider_request
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_vision_request_body_gate_blocks_before_client_construction(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    profile = dict(SEED_20_PROFILE)
    profile["profile_key"] = "fixture-request-body-500-v1"
    profile["max_request_body_bytes"] = 500
    profile["request_body_safety_margin_bytes"] = 0
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'vision-body-gate.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db,
                tmp_path,
                monkeypatch,
                explicit_vision=True,
                vision_profile=profile,
            )

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as excinfo:
                prepare_prompt_invocation(
                    db,
                    run=run,
                    attempt=attempt,
                    node=node,
                    input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
                )

            assert excinfo.value.code == "vision_request_body_too_large"
            assert excinfo.value.retryable is False
            assert excinfo.value.evidence["serialized_request_body_bytes"] > 500
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

def test_real_text_prompt_uses_frozen_context_without_requiring_an_image(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'text-model-executor.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            snapshot_node = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]
            snapshot_node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
            frozen_input = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
            frozen_input.pop("asset_id")
            frozen_input.pop("asset_sha256")
            frozen_input["case_metadata"] = {
                "case_id": "case-42",
                "image_results": [{"score": 91}, {"score": 87}],
            }
            encoded = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_json = encoded
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(encoded)
            db.flush()

            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )

            assert prepared.image_path is None
            assert prepared.mime_type is None
            assert prepared.image_samples is None
            assert prepared.media_bundle.items == ()
            assert prepared.media_dispatch_identities == ()
            assert '"case_id":"case-42"' in prepared.user_prompt
            assert '"score":42' in prepared.user_prompt

            calls: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

            class FakeTextClient:
                def __init__(self, _config):
                    pass

                async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                    calls.append({
                        "system_prompt": system_prompt,
                        "user_prompt": user_prompt,
                        "options": options,
                    })
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 89.0, "label": "通过"},
                        raw_text='{"score":89.0,"label":"通过"}',
                        raw_payload={},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-text-1",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_tokens=90,
                        output_tokens=20,
                        total_tokens=110,
                    )

            result = asyncio.run(
                execute_prepared_prompt(prepared, client_type=FakeTextClient)
            )

            assert result.node_result["output"]["label"] == "通过"
            assert calls[0]["options"]["image_path"] is None
            assert calls[0]["options"]["mime_type"] is None
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_real_prompt_executor_passes_and_enforces_frozen_output_contract(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError,
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'model-output-contract.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db,
                tmp_path,
                monkeypatch,
                strict_output=True,
            )
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )
            captured: dict[str, Any] = {}

            class InvalidSchemaClient:
                def __init__(self, _config):
                    pass

                async def chat_json(self, *_args, **options):
                    captured.update(options)
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 88.5, "label": "好", "extra": True},
                        raw_text='{"score":88.5,"label":"好","extra":true}',
                        raw_payload={},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-schema-mismatch",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_tokens=10,
                        output_tokens=5,
                        total_tokens=15,
                    )

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as exc_info:
                asyncio.run(
                    execute_prepared_prompt(
                        prepared,
                        client_type=InvalidSchemaClient,
                    )
                )

            assert captured["output_contract"].mode == "json_schema"
            assert captured["output_contract"].strict is True
            assert exc_info.value.code == "structured_output_schema_mismatch"
            assert exc_info.value.retryable is True
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_paid_acceptance_campaign_wraps_provider_dispatch_and_settlement(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Catches provider I/O happening outside the campaign-wide call ledger."""

    import app.workflow_model_executor as executor
    from app.models import PaidAcceptanceCallReservation
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'paid-campaign-success.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )
            db.commit()
        monkeypatch.setenv("PAID_ACCEPTANCE_CAMPAIGN_ID", "macbook-20260829")
        monkeypatch.setenv("PAID_ACCEPTANCE_CALL_LIMIT", "2")
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest, raising=False
        )

        class FakeClient:
            def __init__(self, _config):
                pass

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
                return DoubaoResponse(
                    parsed={"score": 88.5, "label": "好"},
                    raw_text='{"score":88.5,"label":"好"}',
                    raw_payload={},
                    upstream_status_code=200,
                    request_correlation_id="req-campaign-safe",
                    attempt_count=1,
                    output_budget=512,
                    reasoning_effort="high",
                    thinking_mode="enabled",
                    input_tokens=10,
                    output_tokens=5,
                    total_tokens=15,
                )

        asyncio.run(
            execute_prepared_prompt(prepared, client_type=FakeClient)
        )

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            reservation = db.scalar(select(PaidAcceptanceCallReservation))
            assert reservation is not None
            assert reservation.attempt_key == f"attempt:{attempt.id}"
            assert reservation.ordinal == 1
            assert reservation.status == "succeeded"
            assert reservation.provider_correlation_id == "req-campaign-safe"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_paid_acceptance_exhaustion_blocks_client_construction(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Catches checking the hard ceiling only after creating a network client."""

    import app.workflow_model_executor as executor
    from app.paid_acceptance_guard import reserve_acceptance_call
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError,
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'paid-campaign-full.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    calls: list[str] = []
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )
            reserve_acceptance_call(
                db,
                campaign_id="macbook-20260829",
                attempt_key="attempt:already-charged",
                limit=1,
            )
            db.commit()
        monkeypatch.setenv("PAID_ACCEPTANCE_CAMPAIGN_ID", "macbook-20260829")
        monkeypatch.setenv("PAID_ACCEPTANCE_CALL_LIMIT", "1")
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest, raising=False
        )

        class MustNotConstructClient:
            def __init__(self, _config):
                calls.append("constructed")

            async def chat_json(self, *_args, **_kwargs):
                raise AssertionError("provider call must be blocked")

        with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as exc_info:
            asyncio.run(
                execute_prepared_prompt(
                    prepared,
                    client_type=MustNotConstructClient,
                )
            )
        assert exc_info.value.code == "acceptance_call_limit_exhausted"
        assert calls == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("input_modality,context_mode", [
    ("image", None), ("text", None), ("image", "full_v1"), ("image", "routing_only_v1"),
])
def test_authoritative_prompt_b_uses_frozen_rules_precheck_and_anchor_images(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
    input_modality: str,
    context_mode: str | None,
) -> None:
    """Native A/B runs must preserve the legacy B prompt and anchor contract."""

    from app.inspiration_category_seed import (
        build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions,
    )
    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        execute_prepared_prompt,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'model-authoritative-b-{input_modality}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)

            node["config"]["asset_key"] = "prompt_b"
            component = node["config"]["component_snapshot"]
            component["version"] = "prompt-b-frozen-v1"
            component["content"] = {
                "stage": "B",
                "system_prompt": "冻结 B 系统正文",
                "user_prompt": (
                    "调用 A：{{precheck_json}}\n"
                    "上一步：{{previous_output}}\n"
                    "规则：{{dimension_rules}}\n"
                    "输出：{{response_contract}}"
                ),
                "rubric_version": "rubric-v7",
            }
            component["content_hash"] = canonical_component_hash(
                component["content"]
            )

            prompt_a_node = deepcopy(node)
            prompt_a_node["node_instance_id"] = "prompt_a"
            prompt_a_node["config"]["asset_key"] = "prompt_a"
            prompt_a_node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content"][
                "stage"
            ] = "A"
            if context_mode is not None:
                node["config"]["precheck_context_mode"] = context_mode

            dimensions = build_inspiration_subcategory_dimensions()
            dimension_content = {"subcategory_dimensions": dimensions}
            dimension_node = {
                "node_instance_id": "dimension_scoring_5",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "config": {
                    "module_type_key": "dimension_scoring",
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "version_id": 501,
                        "version": "17",
                        "module_type_key": "dimension_scoring",
                        "content_json": canonical_json(dimension_content),
                        "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(
                            dimension_content
                        ),
                    },
                },
            }

            anchor_assets = []
            anchors = []
            for index, level in enumerate(("L1", "L2"), start=1):
                path = tmp_path / f"anchor-{index}.jpg"
                Image.new(
                    "RGB",
                    (640 + index, 480 + index),
                    (index, index + 1, index + 2),
                ).save(path, format="JPEG")
                raw = path.read_bytes()
                asset = Asset(
                    original_name=path.name,
                    stored_name=path.name,
                    mime_type="image/jpeg",
                    size_bytes=len(raw),
                    sha256=_sha256(raw),
                    category_key="space_image",
                )
                db.add(asset)
                db.flush()
                anchor_assets.append(asset)
                anchors.append(
                    {
                        "asset_id": asset.id,
                        "level": level,
                        "mime_type": asset.mime_type,
                        "sha256": asset.sha256,
                    }
                )
            anchor_content = {
                "spec_version": "anchor-mechanism-v1",
                "enabled": True,
                "max_anchor_images": 2,
                "anchors": anchors,
            }
            anchor_node = {
                "node_instance_id": "anchor_calibration_6",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "config": {
                    "module_type_key": "anchor_calibration",
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "version_id": 601,
                        "version": "4",
                        "module_type_key": "anchor_calibration",
                        "content_json": canonical_json(anchor_content),
                        "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(anchor_content),
                    },
                },
            }

            prior_output = {
                "track_classification": "class_one",
                "classification_confidence": 0.96,
                "primary_category": "景观设计",
                "redline_triggered": {},
                "reason": ["A独有的主观评语标记"],
                "hard_defects": [],
                "image_defects": [],
                "decisive_evidence": {
                    "redline_triggered": {},
                    "hard_defects": [],
                    "image_defects": [],
                },
                "decision_status": "complete",
                "uncertain_fields": [],
            }
            prior_result = {
                "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                "node_instance_id": "prompt_a",
                "node_kind": "prompt",
                "status": "succeeded",
                "output": prior_output,
            }
            prior_attempt = ProductionStepAttempt(
                run_id=run.id,
                step_key="prompt_a",
                step_type="model_call",
                sequence=0,
                script_version_id=attempt.script_version_id,
                status="succeeded",
                attempt_no=1,
                idempotency_key=f"{run.run_key}:prompt_a:attempt:1",
                input_manifest_json="{}",
                input_hash=_sha256("{}"),
                output_manifest_json=canonical_json(prior_result),
                output_hash=_sha256(canonical_json(prior_result)),
                checkpoint_json="{}",
            )
            db.add(prior_attempt)

            direct_input = {
                "upstream": {
                    "track_routing_2": {
                        "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                        "output": {"track_key": "", "confidence": "low"},
                    }
                }
            }
            attempt.input_manifest_json = canonical_json(direct_input)
            attempt.input_hash = _sha256(attempt.input_manifest_json)
            attempt.sequence = 4

            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["category_key"] = (
                "inspiration_image"
            )
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"] = [
                prompt_a_node,
                node,
                dimension_node,
                anchor_node,
            ]
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["category_key"] = "inspiration_image"
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["real_execution_node_limits"] = {
                "prompt_frozen": snapshot["runtime_context"][
                    "real_execution_node_limits"
                ]["prompt_frozen"]
            }
            run.category_key = "inspiration_image"
            if input_modality == "text":
                node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
                for manifest_node in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]:
                    if manifest_node.get("node_instance_id") == node["node_instance_id"]:
                        manifest_node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
                frozen_input = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
                frozen_input.pop("asset_id")
                frozen_input.pop("asset_sha256")
                frozen_input["case_metadata"] = {"case_id": "text-case-b"}
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            db.flush()

            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=direct_input,
            )

            assert prepared.system_prompt == "冻结 B 系统正文"
            if context_mode == "routing_only_v1":
                assert "routing_only_v1" in prepared.user_prompt
                assert "class_one" in prepared.user_prompt
                assert "A独有的主观评语标记" not in prepared.user_prompt
                assert "景观设计" not in prepared.user_prompt
            else:
                assert "景观设计" in prepared.user_prompt
                assert "A独有的主观评语标记" in prepared.user_prompt
            # Transport projection must not rewrite the authoritative A checkpoint.
            assert prior_attempt.output_manifest_json == canonical_json(prior_result)
            assert prior_attempt.output_hash == _sha256(canonical_json(prior_result))
            assert '"track_key":""' not in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "visual_structure" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "hit_rules" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "{{" not in prepared.user_prompt
            if input_modality == "image":
                assert prepared.image_samples is not None
                assert len(prepared.media_bundle.anchors) == 2
                assert prepared.media_dispatch_identities == (
                    "anchor:0",
                    "anchor:1",
                    "target:0",
                )
                assert [sample[0] for sample in prepared.image_samples] == [
                    f"锚点图 L1（素材 {anchor_assets[0].id}）",
                    f"锚点图 L2（素材 {anchor_assets[1].id}）",
                    "待评图片（禁止把锚点图等级直接当作输出）",
                ]
            else:
                assert prepared.image_samples is None
                assert prepared.media_bundle.items == ()
                assert prepared.media_dispatch_identities == ()

            captured: dict[str, Any] = {}
            cache_usage = CacheUsageObservation(
                status="hit",
                cached_input_tokens=256,
                source="usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
            )

            class FakeAnchorClient:
                def __init__(self, _config):
                    pass

                async def chat_json_images(
                    self, system_prompt, samples, **options
                ):
                    captured.update(
                        {
                            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
                            "samples": samples,
                            "options": options,
                        }
                    )
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 88.5, "label": "好"},
                        raw_text='{"score":88.5,"label":"好"}',
                        raw_payload={"usage": {}},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-anchor-safe",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_image_tokens=101,
                        per_image_tokens=(20, 30, 51),
                        input_tokens=321,
                        output_tokens=45,
                        total_tokens=366,
                        cache_usage=cache_usage,
                    )

                async def chat_json(self, system_prompt, user_prompt, **options):
                    captured.update(
                        {
                            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
                            "samples": None,
                            "options": {**options, "user_prompt": user_prompt},
                        }
                    )
                    return DoubaoResponse(
                        parsed={"score": 88.5, "label": "好"},
                        raw_text='{"score":88.5,"label":"好"}',
                        raw_payload={"usage": {}},
                        upstream_status_code=200,
                        request_correlation_id="req-text-safe",
                        attempt_count=1,
                        output_budget=512,
                        reasoning_effort="high",
                        thinking_mode="enabled",
                        input_tokens=321,
                        output_tokens=45,
                        total_tokens=366,
                        cache_usage=cache_usage,
                    )

            result = asyncio.run(
                execute_prepared_prompt(
                    prepared,
                    client_type=FakeAnchorClient,
                )
            )

            assert captured["samples"] == (
                list(prepared.image_samples)
                if prepared.image_samples is not None
                else None
            )
            assert captured["options"]["user_prompt"] == prepared.user_prompt
            assert result.cache_usage == cache_usage
            assert result.invocation_evidence["cache_observation"] == {
                "status": "hit",
                "cached_input_tokens": 256,
                "source": "usage.prompt_tokens_details.cached_tokens",
            }
            if input_modality == "image":
                assert captured["options"]["max_image_count"] == 3
                assert result.media_usage["tokens_by_identity"] == {
                    "anchor:0": 20,
                    "anchor:1": 30,
                    "target:0": 51,
                }
                assert result.media_usage["total_image_tokens"] == 101
            else:
                assert "tokens_by_identity" not in result.media_usage
                assert "total_image_tokens" not in result.media_usage
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("input_modality", ["image", "text"])
def test_inspiration_prompt_a_receives_the_frozen_redline_fact_contract(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
    input_modality: str,
) -> None:
    """Call A must answer the rule module's current keys, not an old hardcode."""

    from app.workflow_model_executor import prepare_prompt_invocation

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'model-authoritative-a-{input_modality}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            redline_content = {
                "format_version": "redline-policy-v1",
                "enabled": True,
                "hit_level": "L5",
                "hit_score_cap": 49,
                "rules": [
                    {
                        "key": "transparent_checkerboard",
                        "signal": "production_fields.reason",
                        "match_any": ["透明棋盘格"],
                        "exemptions": [],
                        "enabled": True,
                    }
                ],
            }
            redline_node = {
                "node_instance_id": "eligibility_redline_1",
                "kind": "rule_module",
                "config": {
                    "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                    "component_snapshot": {
                        "kind": "rule_module",
                        "version_id": 701,
                        "version": "5",
                        "module_type_key": "eligibility_redline",
                        "content_json": canonical_json(redline_content),
                        "content_hash": canonical_component_hash(redline_content),
                    },
                },
            }
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["category_key"] = (
                "inspiration_image"
            )
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"].append(redline_node)
            snapshot["runtime_context"]["category_key"] = "inspiration_image"
            run.category_key = "inspiration_image"
            if input_modality == "text":
                node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
                snapshot_node = snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]
                snapshot_node["config"]["input_modality"] = "text"
                frozen_input = snapshot["runtime_context"]["input_manifest"]
                frozen_input.pop("asset_id")
                frozen_input.pop("asset_sha256")
                frozen_input["case_metadata"] = {"case_id": "text-case-a"}
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            db.flush()

            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )

            assert "灵感图权威前检合同" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "transparent_checkerboard" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "透明棋盘格" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert "只返回可审计事实，不评分" in prepared.user_prompt
            assert (prepared.image_path is None) is (input_modality == "text")
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_authoritative_prompt_b_rejects_tampered_prompt_a_checkpoint(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """Call B must not consume a succeeded Call-A row whose hash no longer matches."""

    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError,
        _prior_prompt_a_output,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'tampered-prompt-a.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, _model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            prompt_a_node = deepcopy(node)
            prompt_a_node["node_instance_id"] = "prompt_a"
            prompt_a_node["config"]["asset_key"] = "prompt_a"
            prompt_a_node["config"]["component_snapshot"]["content"][
                "stage"
            ] = "A"
            snapshot = json.loads(run.snapshot_json)
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]["config"][
                "asset_key"
            ] = "prompt_b"
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"][0]["config"][
                "component_snapshot"
            ]["content"]["stage"] = "B"
            snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"].append(prompt_a_node)
            run.snapshot_json = canonical_json(snapshot)
            run.snapshot_hash = _sha256(run.snapshot_json)
            result = {
                "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                "node_instance_id": "prompt_a",
                "node_kind": "prompt",
                "status": "succeeded",
                "output": {"track_classification": "class_one"},
            }
            db.add(
                ProductionStepAttempt(
                    run_id=run.id,
                    step_key="prompt_a",
                    step_type="model_call",
                    sequence=0,
                    script_version_id=attempt.script_version_id,
                    status="succeeded",
                    attempt_no=1,
                    idempotency_key=f"{run.run_key}:prompt_a:tampered",
                    input_manifest_json="{}",
                    input_hash=_sha256("{}"),
                    output_manifest_json=canonical_json(result),
                    output_hash="0" * 64,
                    checkpoint_json="{}",
                )
            )
            db.flush()

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as exc_info:
                _prior_prompt_a_output(db, run=run, snapshot=snapshot)

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_authoritative_prompt_a_invalid"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_prepare_prompt_invocation_rejects_model_transport_drift(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """冻结 workflow 不能把当前凭据发送到不同的供应商端点。"""

    from app.workflow_model_executor import (
        WorkflowModelExecutorError,
        prepare_prompt_invocation,
    )

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'model-drift.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            model.base_url = "https://changed-endpoint.invalid"
            db.flush()

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowModelExecutorError) as exc_info:
                prepare_prompt_invocation(
                    db,
                    run=run,
                    attempt=attempt,
                    node=node,
                    input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
                )

            assert exc_info.value.code == "model_config_snapshot_drift"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_prepare_prompt_invocation_uses_frozen_output_params_after_config_changes(
    tmp_path,
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    """评测机制版本应继续使用冻结参数，不受当前全局输出参数变化影响。"""

    from app.workflow_model_executor import prepare_prompt_invocation

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'frozen-output-params.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            run, attempt, node, model, _prompt = _case(
                db, tmp_path, monkeypatch
            )
            model.max_tokens = 8_192
            model.temperature = 0.9
            model.thinking_mode = "disabled"
            db.flush()

            prepared = prepare_prompt_invocation(
                db,
                run=run,
                attempt=attempt,
                node=node,
                input_manifest=json.loads(attempt.input_manifest_json),
            )

            assert prepared.output_budget == 512
            assert prepared.client_config.max_tokens == 512
            assert prepared.client_config.temperature == 0.25
            assert prepared.client_config.thinking_mode == "enabled"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
