def _script(
    db: Session,
    *,
    key: str,
    step_type: str,
    fixture: str,
    artifact: str,
) -> ScriptVersion:
    definition = ScriptDefinition(
        script_key=key,
        name=key,
        description="e2e fixture",
        owner="platform",
        allowed_categories_json='["model_3d_su"]',
        step_types_json=f'["{step_type}"]',
        status="active",
        created_by="e2e",
    )
    db.add(definition)
    db.flush()
    version = ScriptVersion(
        script_definition_id=definition.id,
        version="1",
        display_name=key + " v1",
        executor_kind="deterministic_fixture",
        artifact_sha256=artifact * 64,
        manifest_json='{"fixture":"' + fixture + '"}',
        input_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        output_schema_json='{"type":"object"}',
        required_permissions_json="[]",
        idempotency_template="{run_key}:{step_key}:{input_hash}",
        timeout_seconds=60,
        max_attempts=3,
        retry_policy_json='{"kind":"fixed","delay_seconds":0}',
        concurrency_limit=1,
        estimated_cost_json='{"currency":"CNY","micros":0}',
        status="active",
        validation_report_json='{"ok":true,"errors":[]}',
        blocked_reason="",
        created_by="e2e",
    )
    db.add(version)
    db.flush()
    return version


def exercise_workflow_runtime_two_step_retry(
    engine: Engine,
    *,
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
    identity_suffix: str,
) -> None:
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    identity_key = f"fixture.e2e.identity.{identity_suffix}"
    fail_once_key = f"fixture.e2e.fail-once.{identity_suffix}"
    with SessionForTest() as db:
        identity = _script(
            db,
            key=identity_key,
            step_type="identity",
            fixture="identity",
            artifact="a",
        )
        fail_once = _script(
            db,
            key=fail_once_key,
            step_type="transform",
            fixture="fail_once",
            artifact="b",
        )
        definition = create_workflow_definition(
            db,
            workflow_key=f"label.e2e.retry.{identity_suffix}",
            name="E2E retry",
            description="deterministic dry-run",
            owner="platform",
            allowed_categories=["model_3d_su"],
            created_by="e2e",
        )
        workflow = create_workflow_version(
            db,
            definition=definition,
            version="1",
            manifest={
                "schema_version": "workflow-v1",
                "steps": [
                    {
                        "key": "identity",
                        "type": "identity",
                        "script_version": f"{identity_key}@1",
                        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                    {
                        "key": "retry-proof",
                        "type": "transform",
                        "script_version": f"{fail_once_key}@1",
                        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
                        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                    },
                ],
                "edges": [{"from": "identity", "to": "retry-proof"}],
                "queue_class": "validation",
                "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
                "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
                "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 1},
            },
            created_by="e2e",
        )
        workflow.status = "active"
        workflow.validation_report_json = '{"ok":true,"errors":[]}'
        db.commit()

        request = ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
            idempotency_key=f"runtime-e2e-{identity_suffix}",
            category_key="model_3d_su",
            queue_class="validation",
            runtime_context={"queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"},
            input_manifest={"content_key": "3d:1:42"},
            owner="platform",
            reason="e2e",
        )
        run = create_production_run(db, request, actor="e2e")
        db.commit()
        duplicate = create_production_run(db, request, actor="e2e")
        assert duplicate.id == run.id
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(ProductionRun.id))) == 1
        run_id = run.id

    import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

    monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
    assert process_runtime_step_once(f"e2e-{identity_suffix}-worker") is True
    assert process_runtime_step_once(f"e2e-{identity_suffix}-worker") is True
    assert process_runtime_step_once(f"e2e-{identity_suffix}-worker") is True
    assert process_runtime_step_once(f"e2e-{identity_suffix}-worker") is False

    with SessionForTest() as db:
        persisted = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
        assert persisted is not None
        assert persisted.status == "succeeded"
        assert persisted.completed_steps == 2
        attempts = db.scalars(
            select(ProductionStepAttempt)
            .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
            .order_by(
                ProductionStepAttempt.sequence,
                ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no,
            )
        ).all()
        assert [
            (item.step_key, item.attempt_no, item.status)
            for item in attempts
        ] == [
            ("identity", 1, "succeeded"),
            ("retry-proof", 1, "failed"),
            ("retry-proof", 2, "succeeded"),
        ]
        assert sum(item.checkpoint_hash is not None for item in attempts) == 2
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(EvaluationJob.id))) == 0
        assert db.scalar(select(func.count(LabelRelease.id))) == 0
        queue_classes = set(
            db.scalars(select(RuntimeDispatchItem.queue_class)).all()
        )
        assert queue_classes == {"validation", "recovery"}
        scheduler = db.get(QueueSchedulerState, 1)
        assert scheduler is not None
        assert scheduler.dispatch_count == 3
        assert identity.id != fail_once.id


def test_workflow_runtime_two_step_retry_dry_run(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'workflow-runtime-e2e.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        exercise_workflow_runtime_two_step_retry(
            engine,
            monkeypatch=monkeypatch,
            identity_suffix="sqlite",
        )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_workflow_v3_repeated_module_instances_execute_from_frozen_snapshot(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'workflow-v3-runtime-e2e.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            definition, manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            rule_asset = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == "space_image",
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.kind == "rule_module",
                )
            )
            assert rule_asset is not None
            rule_working = get_component_working_copy(db, rule_asset.id)
            second_rule_content = json.loads(rule_working.content_json)
            second_rule_content["hit_score_cap"] = 40
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=rule_asset.id,
                expected_lock_version=rule_working.lock_version,
                content=second_rule_content,
                updated_by="ops",
            )
            second_rule = generate_component_version(
                db, asset_id=rule_asset.id, created_by="ops"
            )

            first_rule_node = next(
                node for node in manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "rule_module"
            )
            second_rule_node = deepcopy(first_rule_node)
            second_rule_node["node_instance_id"] = "redline_2"
            second_rule_node["display_name"] = "再次执行准入与红线"
            second_rule_node["config"]["rule_module_version_id"] = second_rule.id
            second_rule_node["config"]["input_bindings"] = {
                "previous_rule_result": "redline_1.output"
            }
            manifest["nodes"].insert(-1, second_rule_node)
            manifest["edges"] = [
                {"from": "upstream", "to": "prompt_1"},
                {"from": "prompt_1", "to": "redline_1"},
                {"from": "redline_1", "to": "redline_2"},
                {"from": "redline_2", "to": "downstream"},
            ]
            get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                initial_manifest=manifest,
                actor="ops",
            )
            workflow = generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            request = ProductionRunRequest(
                workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                idempotency_key="runtime-v3-repeated-modules",
                category_key="space_image",
                queue_class="validation",
                runtime_context={"queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"},
                input_manifest={
                    "content_key": "space:1:42",
                    "request_id": "request-42",
                    "trace_id": "trace-42",
                },
                owner="platform",
                reason="workflow-v3 fixture regression",
            )
            run = create_production_run(db, request, actor="ops")
            frozen_snapshot = run.snapshot_json

            prompt_asset = db.scalar(
                select(WorkflowNodeAsset).where(
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.category_key == "space_image",
                    WorkflowNodeAsset.kind == "prompt",
                )
            )
            assert prompt_asset is not None
            prompt_working = get_component_working_copy(db, prompt_asset.id)
            changed_prompt = json.loads(prompt_working.content_json)
            changed_prompt["system_prompt"] = "这是排队后才修改的提示词。"
            save_component_working_copy(
                db,
                asset_id=prompt_asset.id,
                expected_lock_version=prompt_working.lock_version,
                content=changed_prompt,
                updated_by="another-operator",
            )
            db.commit()
            db.refresh(run)
            assert run.snapshot_json == frozen_snapshot
            run_id = run.id

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once("v3-e2e-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 10
        assert processed == 5

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            persisted = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert persisted is not None
            assert persisted.status == "succeeded"
            assert persisted.snapshot_json == frozen_snapshot
            snapshot = json.loads(persisted.snapshot_json)
            frozen_nodes = {
                node["node_instance_id"]: node
                for node in snapshot["workflow"]["manifest"]["nodes"]
            }
            attempts = {
                attempt.step_key: attempt
                for attempt in db.scalars(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id,
                        ProductionStepAttempt.status == "succeeded",
                    )
                ).all()
            }
            assert {"redline_1", "redline_2"} <= set(attempts)
            for node_instance_id in ("redline_1", "redline_2"):
                attempt = attempts[node_instance_id]
                output = json.loads(attempt.output_manifest_json)
                config = frozen_nodes[node_instance_id]["config"]
                assert output["node_instance_id"] == node_instance_id
                assert output["input_fingerprint"] == attempt.input_hash
                assert output["component_version_id"] == config[
                    "rule_module_version_id"
                ]
                assert output["component_hash"] == config["component_snapshot"][
                    "content_hash"
                ]
                assert output["module_type_key"] == "eligibility_redline"
                assert output["output"] == {
                    "hit": False,
                    "matched_rules": [],
                    "level_cap": None,
                    "reason": "",
                }
                assert isinstance(output["evidence"], dict)
                assert output["attempt"] == 1
                assert output["started_at"]
                assert output["finished_at"]
            assert (
                json.loads(attempts["redline_1"].output_manifest_json)[
                    "component_version_id"
                ]
                != json.loads(attempts["redline_2"].output_manifest_json)[
                    "component_version_id"
                ]
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_workflow_v3_aggregate_case_executes_from_frozen_foreach_output(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'aggregate-case-e2e.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            definition, base_manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            prompt = next(
                node for node in base_manifest["nodes"] if node["kind"] == "prompt"
            )
            executor = dict(prompt["config"])
            executor.update(
                {
                    "kind": "prompt",
                    "input_modality": "image",
                    "thinking_level": "none",
                    "thinking_enabled": False,
                    "structured_output": True,
                    "output_contract": {
                        "mode": "json_schema",
                        "name": "case_image_result",
                        "description": "案例单图结果",
                        "strict": True,
                        "schema": {
                            "type": "object",
                            "properties": {"score": {"type": "number"}},
                            "required": ["score"],
                            "additionalProperties": False,
                        },
                    },
                }
            )
            model = db.get(ModelRegistryEntry, executor["model_registry_id"])
            assert model is not None
            source_model = db.get(ModelConfig, model.source_model_config_id)
            assert source_model is not None
            model.protocol = "openai_chat"
            source_model.protocol = "openai_chat"
            manifest = {
                **base_manifest,
                "nodes": [
                    _node(
                        "foreach_images",
                        "foreach_image",
                        {
                            "items_path": "$.images",
                            "item_identity_path": "$.image_id",
                            "max_items": 100,
                            "max_parallel_items": 4,
                            "failure_policy": "fail_open",
                            "item_executor": executor,
                        },
                    ),
                    _node(
                        "aggregate_case",
                        "aggregate_case",
                        {
                            "foreach_node_id": "foreach_images",
                            "included_statuses": ["succeeded"],
                            "score_path": "$.score",
                            "feature_paths": {"trait": "$.trait"},
                            "empty_policy": "fail",
                            "failure_projection": "include",
                        },
                    ),
                ],
                "edges": [{"from": "foreach_images", "to": "aggregate_case"}],
            }
            get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                initial_manifest=manifest,
                actor="ops",
            )
            workflow = generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                    idempotency_key="aggregate-case-runtime-e2e",
                    category_key="space_image",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={"queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"},
                    input_manifest={
                        "images": [
                            {"image_id": "a"},
                            {"image_id": "b"},
                        ]
                    },
                    owner="platform",
                    reason="aggregate case e2e",
                ),
                actor="ops",
            )
            db.commit()
            root_attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(
                db,
                "aggregate-case-seed-worker",
                global_limit=1,
            )
            assert root_attempt_id is not None
            root_attempt = db.get(ProductionStepAttempt, root_attempt_id)
            assert root_attempt is not None and root_attempt.lease_token
            complete_runtime_step(
                db,
                root_attempt.id,
                root_attempt.lease_token,
                {
                    "schema_version": "workflow-foreach-output-v1",
                    "node_instance_id": "foreach_images",
                    "source_hash": "frozen-source",
                    "item_contract_hash": "frozen-contract",
                    "counts": {
                        "total": 2,
                        "succeeded": 2,
                        "failed": 0,
                        "skipped": 0,
                    },
                    "items": [
                        {
                            "item_key": "b",
                            "ordinal": 1,
                            "status": "succeeded",
                            "attempt_count": 1,
                            "input_hash": "input-b",
                            "output_hash": "output-b",
                            "output": {"score": 60, "trait": "实景照片"},
                        },
                        {
                            "item_key": "a",
                            "ordinal": 0,
                            "status": "succeeded",
                            "attempt_count": 1,
                            "input_hash": "input-a",
                            "output_hash": "output-a",
                            "output": {"score": 80, "trait": "效果图"},
                        },
                    ],
                },
            )
            db.commit()
            run_id = run.id

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor_module

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor_module, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
        assert process_runtime_step_once("aggregate-case-worker") is True
        assert process_runtime_step_once("aggregate-case-worker") is False

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            persisted = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert persisted is not None
            assert persisted.status == "succeeded"
            aggregate_attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id,
                    ProductionStepAttempt.step_key == "aggregate_case",
                    ProductionStepAttempt.status == "succeeded",
                )
            )
            assert aggregate_attempt is not None
            output = json.loads(aggregate_attempt.output_manifest_json)
            assert output["schema_version"] == "workflow-node-result-v1"
            assert output["status"] == "succeeded"
            assert output["output"]["schema_version"] == (
                "workflow-case-aggregate-v1"
            )
            assert output["output"]["score"]["mean"] == 70.0
            assert [
                item["item_key"] for item in output["output"]["results"]
            ] == ["a", "b"]
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _exclusive_runtime_manifest(
    *,
    domestic_value: str = "space:route:1",
    overseas_value: str = "not-space:route:1",
) -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": "workflow-v3",
        "category_key": "space_image",
        "lane_key": "evaluation",
        "run_modes": ["evaluation", "stock_regression"],
        "nodes": [
            _node(
                "upstream",
                "upstream_connector",
                {"connector_key": "logical.fixture.input"},
            ),
            _node(
                "split",
                "branch",
                {
                    "branch_key": "source",
                    "branches": ["domestic", "overseas"],
                    "mode": "exclusive",
                },
            ),
            _node(
                "route_domestic",
                "upstream_connector",
                {"connector_key": "logical.fixture.domestic"},
            ),
            _node(
                "route_overseas",
                "upstream_connector",
                {"connector_key": "logical.fixture.overseas"},
            ),
            _node("join", "join", {"join_for": "source", "mode": "all"}),
            _node(
                "downstream",
                "downstream_projection",
                {"connector_key": "logical.fixture.output"},
            ),
        ],
        "edges": [
            {"from": "upstream", "to": "split"},
            {
                "from": "split",
                "to": "route_domestic",
                "branch": "domestic",
                "condition": {
                    "op": "eq",
                    "path": "steps.upstream.output.content_key",
                    "value": domestic_value,
                },
            },
            {
                "from": "split",
                "to": "route_overseas",
                "branch": "overseas",
                "condition": {
                    "op": "eq",
                    "path": "steps.upstream.output.content_key",
                    "value": overseas_value,
                },
            },
            {"from": "route_domestic", "to": "join"},
            {"from": "route_overseas", "to": "join"},
            {"from": "join", "to": "downstream"},
        ],
        "queue_class": "validation",
        "input_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "output_schema": {"type": "object"},
        "resource_policy": {"max_parallel": 2},
    }


def _create_exclusive_runtime(
    db: Session,
    *,
    manifest: dict,
    idempotency_key: str,
) -> ProductionRun:
    definition, _ = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
    get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(
        db,
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        initial_manifest=manifest,
        actor="ops",
    )
    workflow = generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
        db,
        workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
        expected_lock_version=1,
        created_by="ops",
    )
    return create_production_run(
        db,
        ProductionRunRequest(
            workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
            idempotency_key=idempotency_key,
            category_key="space_image",
            queue_class="validation",
            runtime_context={"queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"},
            input_manifest={"content_key": "space:route:1"},
            owner="platform",
            reason="exclusive route e2e",
        ),
        actor="ops",
    )


def test_workflow_v3_exclusive_route_skips_unselected_path_and_joins(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path
) -> None:
    """A runtime regression must not execute or wait for an unselected route."""

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'exclusive-route-e2e.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run = _create_exclusive_runtime(
                db,
                manifest=_exclusive_runtime_manifest(),
                idempotency_key="exclusive-route-domestic",
            )
            db.commit()
            run_id = run.id

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once("exclusive-route-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 8

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            persisted = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert persisted is not None
            assert persisted.status == "succeeded"
            assert persisted.completed_steps == persisted.total_steps == 6
            latest = {
                attempt.step_key: attempt
                for attempt in db.scalars(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                    .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
                    .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc())
                ).all()
            }
            assert latest["route_domestic"].status == "succeeded"
            assert latest["route_overseas"].status == "skipped"
            assert latest["route_overseas"].last_error_code == "route_not_selected"
            assert latest["route_overseas"].checkpoint_hash
            skipped_output = json.loads(
                latest["route_overseas"].output_manifest_json
            )
            assert skipped_output == {
                "schema_version": "workflow-node-result-v1",
                "node_instance_id": "route_overseas",
                "node_kind": "upstream_connector",
                "status": "skipped",
                "upstream_refs": ["split"],
                "skipped_reason": "route_not_selected",
                "route_decision_ref": "split",
            }
            split_output = json.loads(latest["split"].output_manifest_json)
            assert split_output["route_decision"]["activated_branches"] == [
                "domestic"
            ]
            assert split_output["route_decision"]["skipped_branches"] == [
                "overseas"
            ]
            join_output = json.loads(latest["join"].output_manifest_json)
            assert join_output["output"]["skipped_predecessors"] == [
                "route_overseas"
            ]
            assert processed == 5
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("domestic_value", "overseas_value", "error_code"),
    [
        ("missing-a", "missing-b", "exclusive_route_no_match"),
        ("space:route:1", "space:route:1", "exclusive_route_multiple_matches"),
    ],
)
def test_workflow_v3_exclusive_route_fails_closed_when_match_is_ambiguous(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    domestic_value: str,
    overseas_value: str,
    error_code: str,
) -> None:
    """Zero or multiple matches must terminate without dispatching a route."""

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / ('exclusive-route-' + error_code + '.db')}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run = _create_exclusive_runtime(
                db,
                manifest=_exclusive_runtime_manifest(
                    domestic_value=domestic_value,
                    overseas_value=overseas_value,
                ),
                idempotency_key=error_code,
            )
            db.commit()
            run_id = run.id

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once("exclusive-route-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 4

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            persisted = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert persisted is not None
            assert persisted.status == "failed"
            assert persisted.error_code == error_code
            latest = {
                attempt.step_key: attempt
                for attempt in db.scalars(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                    .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
                    .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no.desc())
                ).all()
            }
            assert latest["split"].status == "failed"
            assert latest["route_domestic"].status == "skipped"
            assert latest["route_overseas"].status == "skipped"
            assert processed == 2
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_workflow_v3_exclusive_route_can_stop_for_manual_review(
    monkeypatch, tmp_path
) -> None:
    """Manual-review policy must block instead of guessing or failing the run."""

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'exclusive-route-manual.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        manifest = _exclusive_runtime_manifest(
            domestic_value="missing-a",
            overseas_value="missing-b",
        )
        split = next(
            node
            for node in manifest["nodes"]
            if node["node_instance_id"] == "split"
        )
        split["error_policy"] = {"kind": "manual_review"}
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run = _create_exclusive_runtime(
                db,
                manifest=manifest,
                idempotency_key="exclusive-route-manual-review",
            )
            db.commit()
            run_id = run.id

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
        assert process_runtime_step_once("exclusive-route-worker") is True
        assert process_runtime_step_once("exclusive-route-worker") is True
        assert process_runtime_step_once("exclusive-route-worker") is False

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            persisted = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert persisted is not None
            assert persisted.status == "blocked"
            assert persisted.error_code == "exclusive_route_no_match"
            latest = {
                attempt.step_key: attempt
                for attempt in db.scalars(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id
                    )
                ).all()
            }
            assert latest["split"].status == "blocked"
            assert latest["route_domestic"].status == "pending"
            assert latest["route_overseas"].status == "pending"
            blockers = json.loads(persisted.blockers_json)
            assert blockers == [
                {
                    "code": "exclusive_route_no_match",
                    "message": "按条件选择路线时没有任何路线命中，已停止运行",
                    "step_key": "split",
                }
            ]
            with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as resume_error:
                resume_from_checkpoint(db, run_id)
            assert resume_error.value.code == "run_manual_review_required"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("policy_kind", ["continue", "skip"])
def test_workflow_v3_error_policy_records_failure_and_continues_downstream(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
    policy_kind: str,
) -> None:
    """A declared fail-open policy must not silently terminalize the run."""

    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'error-policy-{policy_kind}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        manifest = _exclusive_runtime_manifest(
            domestic_value="missing-a",
            overseas_value="missing-b",
        )
        split = next(
            node
            for node in manifest["nodes"]
            if node["node_instance_id"] == "split"
        )
        split["error_policy"] = {"kind": policy_kind}
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run = _create_exclusive_runtime(
                db,
                manifest=manifest,
                idempotency_key=f"exclusive-route-{policy_kind}",
            )
            db.commit()
            run_id = run.id

        import app.workflow_fixture_executor as executor

        monkeypatch.setattr(executor, "SessionLocal", SessionForTest)
        processed = 0
        while process_runtime_step_once("error-policy-worker"):
            processed += 1
            assert processed <= 8

        with SessionForTest() as db:
            persisted = db.get(ProductionRun, run_id)
            assert persisted is not None
            assert persisted.status == "succeeded"
            latest = {
                attempt.step_key: attempt
                for attempt in db.scalars(
                    select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id
                    )
                ).all()
            }
            failed_open = latest["split"]
            assert failed_open.status == "skipped"
            assert failed_open.last_error_code == f"error_policy_{policy_kind}"
            evidence = json.loads(failed_open.output_manifest_json)
            assert evidence == {
                "error": {
                    "code": "exclusive_route_no_match",
                    "message": "按条件选择路线时没有任何路线命中，已停止运行",
                },
                "error_policy": policy_kind,
                "schema_version": "workflow-error-policy-evidence-v1",
                "status": "skipped",
                "step_key": "split",
            }
            assert latest["downstream"].status == "succeeded"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def _single_node_retry_manifest(retry_policy: dict, *, error_policy: str = "fail") -> dict:
    manifest = _exclusive_runtime_manifest()
    node = deepcopy(manifest["nodes"][0])
    node["retry_policy"] = retry_policy
    node["error_policy"] = {"kind": error_policy}
    manifest["nodes"] = [node]
    manifest["edges"] = []
    return manifest


@pytest.mark.parametrize("delay_seconds", [float("inf"), float("nan"), 1e308])
def test_runtime_rejects_non_finite_or_unbounded_retry_delay(
    delay_seconds: float,
) -> None:
    from app.workflow_runtime import _retry_policy_from_node

    with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as exc_info:
        _retry_policy_from_node(
            {
                "retry_policy": {
                    "kind": "exponential",
                    "max_attempts": 5,
                    "delay_seconds": delay_seconds,
                }
            },
            fallback_max_attempts=3,
        )

    assert exc_info.value.code == "runtime_retry_policy_invalid"


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("retry_policy", "expected_delay", "expected_limit"),
    [
        ({"kind": "none"}, None, 1),
        ({"kind": "fixed", "max_attempts": 3, "delay_seconds": 5}, 5, 3),
        (
            {"kind": "exponential", "max_attempts": 3, "delay_seconds": 5},
            5,
            3,
        ),
    ],
)
def test_workflow_v3_retry_policy_controls_runtime_attempts_and_delay(
    tmp_path,
    retry_policy: dict,
    expected_delay: int | None,
    expected_limit: int,
) -> None:
    filename = f"retry-{retry_policy['kind']}.db"
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / filename}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    first_failure_at = datetime(2026, 9, 1, 0, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run = _create_exclusive_runtime(
                db,
                manifest=_single_node_retry_manifest(retry_policy),
                idempotency_key=f"retry-policy-{retry_policy['kind']}",
            )
            attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id
                )
            )
            dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
                )
            )
            assert attempt is not None and dispatch is not None
            run.status = "running"
            attempt.status = "running"
            attempt.lease_token = "retry-policy-lease-1"
            dispatch.status = "leased"
            dispatch.lease_token = attempt.lease_token

            result = fail_runtime_step(
                db,
                attempt.id,
                attempt.lease_token,
                "temporary_failure",
                "temporary failure",
                retryable=True,
                now=first_failure_at,
            )

            if retry_policy["kind"] == "none":
                assert result.id == attempt.id
                assert run.status == "failed"
                assert db.scalar(
                    select(func.count(ProductionStepAttempt.id)).where(
                        ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id
                    )
                ) == 1
                assert dispatch.max_attempts == expected_limit
                return

            assert result.attempt_no == 2
            next_dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == result.id
                )
            )
            assert next_dispatch is not None
            assert next_dispatch.max_attempts == expected_limit
            next_available_at = next_dispatch.available_at
            if next_available_at.tzinfo is None:
                next_available_at = next_available_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            assert next_available_at == first_failure_at + timedelta(
                seconds=expected_delay
            )

            second_failure_at = first_failure_at + timedelta(seconds=30)
            run.status = "running"
            result.status = "running"
            result.lease_token = "retry-policy-lease-2"
            next_dispatch.status = "leased"
            next_dispatch.lease_token = result.lease_token
            third = fail_runtime_step(
                db,
                result.id,
                result.lease_token,
                "temporary_failure",
                "temporary failure",
                retryable=True,
                now=second_failure_at,
            )
            third_dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == third.id
                )
            )
            assert third_dispatch is not None
            second_delay = 10 if retry_policy["kind"] == "exponential" else 5
            third_available_at = third_dispatch.available_at
            if third_available_at.tzinfo is None:
                third_available_at = third_available_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
            assert third_available_at == second_failure_at + timedelta(
                seconds=second_delay
            )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_expired_lease_exhaustion_honors_continue_policy(tmp_path) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'lease-continue.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    now = datetime(2026, 9, 1, 1, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run = _create_exclusive_runtime(
                db,
                manifest=_single_node_retry_manifest(
                    {"kind": "none"},
                    error_policy="continue",
                ),
                idempotency_key="expired-lease-continue",
            )
            attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id
                )
            )
            dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
                )
            )
            assert attempt is not None and dispatch is not None
            run.status = "running"
            attempt.status = "running"
            attempt.lease_token = "expired-continue-lease"
            attempt.lease_expires_at = now - timedelta(seconds=1)
            dispatch.status = "leased"
            dispatch.lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            dispatch.lease_expires_at = attempt.lease_expires_at
            db.flush()

            assert recover_expired_runtime_steps(db, now=now) == 1

            db.refresh(run)
            db.refresh(attempt)
            assert attempt.status == "skipped"
            assert attempt.last_error_code == "error_policy_continue"
            assert run.status == "succeeded"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("policy_kind", ["continue", "skip"])
def test_expired_reserved_model_invocation_honors_fail_open_policy(
    tmp_path,
    policy_kind: str,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(
        f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / f'unknown-model-{policy_kind}.db'}"
    )
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    now = datetime(2026, 9, 1, 1, 0, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            run = _create_exclusive_runtime(
                db,
                manifest=_single_node_retry_manifest(
                    {"kind": "none"},
                    error_policy=policy_kind,
                ),
                idempotency_key=f"unknown-model-{policy_kind}",
            )
            attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run.id
                )
            )
            dispatch = db.scalar(
                select(RuntimeDispatchItem).where(
                    RuntimeDispatchItem.step_attempt_id == attempt.id
                )
            )
            assert attempt is not None and dispatch is not None
            run.status = "running"
            attempt.status = "running"
            attempt.lease_token = f"unknown-{policy_kind}-lease"
            attempt.lease_expires_at = now - timedelta(seconds=1)
            dispatch.status = "leased"
            dispatch.lease_token = attempt.lease_token
            dispatch.lease_expires_at = attempt.lease_expires_at
            invocation = WorkflowModelInvocation(
                baseline_run_id=None,
                production_run_id=run.id,
                step_attempt_id=attempt.id,
                node_instance_id=attempt.step_key,
                status="reserved",
                provider="doubao",
                model_id="unknown-outcome-model",
                reserved_input_tokens=100,
                reserved_output_tokens=50,
                estimated_cost_micros=20,
                evidence_json=(
                    '{"schema_version":"workflow-model-invocation-v1"}'
                ),
            )
            db.add(invocation)
            db.flush()

            assert recover_expired_runtime_steps(db, now=now) == 1

            db.refresh(run)
            db.refresh(attempt)
            db.refresh(dispatch)
            db.refresh(invocation)
            assert invocation.status == "failed"
            assert invocation.error_code == "model_invocation_outcome_unknown"
            assert attempt.status == "skipped"
            assert attempt.last_error_code == f"error_policy_{policy_kind}"
            assert dispatch.status == "completed"
            assert dispatch.last_error_code == f"error_policy_{policy_kind}"
            assert run.status == "succeeded"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_workflow_v3_run_creation_rejects_tampered_executable_fields(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    """A persisted active row must still pass the executor gate before dispatch."""

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'executor-preflight.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    SessionForTest = sessionmaker(bind=engine, expire_on_commit=False)
    try:
        with SessionForTest() as db:
            definition, manifest = _seed_managed_dependencies(db)
            get_or_create_workflow_working_copy(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                initial_manifest=manifest,
                actor="ops",
            )
            workflow = generate_evaluation_mechanism_version(
                db,
                workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                expected_lock_version=1,
                created_by="ops",
            )
            nodes = json.loads(workflow.step_manifest_json)
            prompt = next(node for node in nodes if node["kind"] == "prompt")
            prompt["config"]["sql"] = "select * from secrets"
            workflow.step_manifest_json = json.dumps(nodes)
            db.flush()

            with pytest.raises(WorkflowRuntimeError) as error:
                create_production_run(
                    db,
                    ProductionRunRequest(
                        workflow_version_id=workflow.id,
                        idempotency_key="tampered-executor-gate",
                        category_key="space_image",
                        queue_class="validation",
                        runtime_context={"queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"},
                        input_manifest={"content_key": "space:1:42"},
                        owner="platform",
                        reason="executor preflight",
                    ),
                    actor="ops",
                )

            assert error.value.code == "arbitrary_code_field_forbidden"
            assert db.scalars(select(ProductionRun)).all() == []
            assert db.scalars(select(ProductionStepAttempt)).all() == []
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

import pytest

RUNTIME_MODELS = (

def test_runtime_models_are_registered_with_expected_tables(tmp_path) -> None:
    for name in RUNTIME_MODELS:
        assert hasattr(models, name), f"missing ORM model {name}"

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'runtime-models.db'}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        tables = set(inspect(engine).get_table_names())
        assert {
            "script_definitions",
            "script_versions",
            "workflow_definitions",
            "workflow_versions",
            "production_runs",
            "production_step_attempts",
            "workflow_foreach_items",
            "workflow_model_invocations",
            "runtime_dispatch_items",
            "runtime_audit_events",
            "workflow_snapshot_blobs",
            "runtime_outbox_events",
            "evaluation_component_working_copies",
            "rule_module_versions",
            "workflow_working_copies",
            "category_incremental_mechanism_bindings",
            "category_incremental_mechanism_events",
            "legacy_evaluation_revision_mappings",
            "baseline_correction_component_candidates",
        } <= tables
        run_columns = {
            row["name"] for row in inspect(engine).get_columns("production_runs")
        }
        assert "snapshot_blob_id" in run_columns
        dispatch_columns = {
            row["name"]
            for row in inspect(engine).get_columns("runtime_dispatch_items")
        }
        assert {
            "attempt_count",
            "max_attempts",
            "lease_owner",
            "lease_token",
            "leased_at",
            "heartbeat_at",
            "lease_expires_at",
            "last_error_code",
            "last_error_message",
            "dead_lettered_at",
        } <= dispatch_columns
        package_columns = {
            row["name"] for row in inspect(engine).get_columns("material_packages")
        }
        assert {"workflow_version_id", "mechanism_snapshot_json"} <= package_columns
        invocation_columns = {
            row["name"]
            for row in inspect(engine).get_columns("workflow_model_invocations")
        }
        assert "invocation_key" in invocation_columns
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_native_evaluation_compatibility_models_expose_workflow_identity(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'native-evaluation-facts.db'}")
    try:
        Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
        inspector = inspect(engine)

        expected_columns = {
            "baseline_regression_runs": {
                "workflow_version_id",
                "execution_engine",
            },
            "baseline_set_items": {
                "expected_result_json",
                "truth_contract_key",
            },
            "baseline_regression_items": {
                "expected_result_json",
                "truth_contract_key",
                "production_run_id",
            },
            "evaluation_jobs": {"workflow_version_id", "production_run_id"},
            "evaluation_results": {
                "workflow_version_id",
                "production_run_id",
                "workflow_snapshot_hash",
                "workflow_node_outputs_json",
                "normalized_result_json",
            },
            "baseline_correction_runs": {"candidate_workflow_version_id"},
        }
        for table, names in expected_columns.items():
            actual = {row["name"] for row in inspector.get_columns(table)}
            assert names <= actual, f"{table} missing {sorted(names - actual)}"

        nullability = {
            row["name"]: row["nullable"]
            for row in inspector.get_columns("baseline_regression_runs")
        }
        assert nullability["strategy_bundle_id"] is True
        for table in ("baseline_set_items", "baseline_regression_items"):
            columns = {
                row["name"]: row["nullable"]
                for row in inspector.get_columns(table)
            }
            assert columns["expected_level"] is True

        with engine.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "INSERT INTO baseline_sets "
                "(id, category_key, name, description, default_expected_level, "
                "fingerprint, created_by, created_at) VALUES "
                "(1, 'model_3d_su', 'native-contract', '', 'L1', ?, 'test', "
                "CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)",
                ("a" * 64,),
            )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                connection.exec_driver_sql(
                    "INSERT INTO baseline_regression_runs "
                    "(baseline_set_id, sequence_no, strategy_bundle_id, "
                    "category_key, strategy_snapshot_json, execution_snapshot_json, "
                    "baseline_set_fingerprint, execution_engine, status, total, "
                    "completed, valid_predictions, failed, metrics_json, created_by, "
                    "created_at) VALUES "
                    "(1, 1, NULL, 'model_3d_su', '{}', '{}', ?, "
                    "'native_workflow', 'running', 0, 0, 0, 0, '{}', 'test', "
                    "CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)",
                    ("a" * 64,),
                )
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'incremental-binding.db'}")

                workflow_key="space-image-evaluation",
                name="空间图片评测",
                owner="evaluation-platform",
                created_by="test",
            )
            db.add(definition)
            db.flush()
            versions = [
                models.WorkflowVersion(
                    workflow_definition_id=definition.id,
                    version=version,
                    step_manifest_json="[]",
                    edge_manifest_json="[]",
                    input_schema_json="{}",
                    output_schema_json="{}",
                    canonical_hash=digest,
                    created_by="test",
                )
                for version, digest in (("1", "a" * 64), ("2", "b" * 64))

            db.flush()
            db.add_all(
                [
                    models.CategoryIncrementalMechanismBinding(
                        category_key="space_image",

                        lock_version=1,

                        category_key="space_image",
                        workflow_version_id=versions[1].id,
                        lock_version=1,
                        updated_by="b",
                    ),
                ]
            )
            with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
                db.flush()
    finally:
        engine.dispose()

===== FILE backend/tests/test_workflow_runtime_recovery.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import importlib
import importlib.util
import asyncio
import json
import threading
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone

import pytest

from sqlalchemy import create_engine, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.database import Base
from app.models import (
    ModelConfig,
    ModelQuotaPool,
    ProductionRun,
    ProductionStepAttempt,
    QueueSchedulerState,
    ScriptVersion,
    WorkflowModelInvocation,
)
from app.workflow_runtime import (
    ProductionRunRequest,
    claim_next_runtime_step,
    create_production_run,
    recover_expired_runtime_steps,
)
from tests.test_workflow_runtime import _seed_active_workflow
from app import worker


def _fixture_module():
    assert importlib.util.find_spec("app.workflow_fixture_executor") is not None
    return importlib.import_module("app.workflow_fixture_executor")


def test_process_runtime_step_once_retries_fixture_failure(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'fixture-runtime.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
            script = db.scalar(select(ScriptVersion))
            assert script is not None
            script.manifest_json = json.dumps({"fixture": "fail_once"})
            db.commit()
            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                    idempotency_key="fixture-run",
                    category_key="model_3d_su",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={"queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"},
                    input_manifest={"content_key": "3d:1:42"},
                    owner="platform",
                    reason="fixture",
                ),
                actor="admin",
            )
            run_id = run.id
            db.commit()

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", lambda: Session(engine))
        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once("fixture-worker") is True

        with Session(engine) as db:
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                    .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
            ).all()
            assert [attempt.status for attempt in attempts] == [
                "failed",
                "retryable",
            ]
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).status == "retryable"

        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once("fixture-worker") is True

        with Session(engine) as db:
            attempts = db.scalars(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt)
                    .where(ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id)
                .order_by(ProductionStepAttempt.attempt_no)
            ).all()
            assert attempts[-1].status == "succeeded"
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run_id).status == "succeeded"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_preflight_failure_evidence_is_persisted_without_an_invocation(
    monkeypatch,
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'preflight-evidence.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                    idempotency_key="preflight-evidence",
                    category_key="model_3d_su",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={
                        "queue_policy_version": "queue-policy-v1"
                    },
                    input_manifest={"content_key": "3d:preflight"},
                    owner="platform",
                    reason="fixture",
                ),
                actor="admin",
            )
            run_id = run.id
            db.commit()

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", lambda: Session(engine))

        def fail_before_dispatch(*_args, **_kwargs):
            raise fixture.FixtureExecutionError(
                "vision_request_body_too_large",
                "模型请求体超过安全上限",
                retryable=False,
                evidence={
                    "serialized_request_body_bytes": 501,
                    "operational_max_bytes": 500,
                },
            )

        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "execute_fixture", fail_before_dispatch)

        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once("preflight-worker") is True

        with Session(engine) as db:
            attempt = db.scalar(
                select(ProductionStepAttempt).where(
                    ProductionStepAttempt.run_id == run_id
                )
            )
            assert attempt is not None
            checkpoint = json.loads(attempt.checkpoint_json)
            assert checkpoint["status"] == "failed_preflight"
            assert checkpoint["failure"] == {
                "code": "vision_request_body_too_large",
                "evidence": {
                    "serialized_request_body_bytes": 501,
                    "operational_max_bytes": 500,
                },
            }
            assert attempt.checkpoint_hash == fixture.hash_manifest(checkpoint)
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_complete_runtime_step_retries_mysql_deadlock_in_a_fresh_transaction(
    monkeypatch,
) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    events: list[str] = []
    completion_attempts = 0

    class MysqlDeadlock(RuntimeError):
        def __init__(self) -> None:
            super().__init__(1213, "Deadlock found when trying to get lock")

    class FakeSession:
        def commit(self) -> None:
            events.append("commit")

        def rollback(self) -> None:
            events.append("rollback")

        def close(self) -> None:
            events.append("close")

    def complete(_db, _attempt_id, _lease_token, _output_manifest) -> None:
        nonlocal completion_attempts
        completion_attempts += 1
        events.append(f"complete:{completion_attempts}")
        if completion_attempts == 1:
            raise MysqlDeadlock()

    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", FakeSession)
    monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "complete_runtime_step", complete)
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        fixture.time,
        "sleep",
        lambda seconds: events.append(f"sleep:{seconds}"),
    )

    fixture._complete_runtime_step_with_retry(
        attempt_id=42,
        lease_token="lease-token",
        output_manifest={"ok": True},
    )

    assert completion_attempts == 2
    assert events == [
        "complete:1",
        "rollback",
        "close",
        "sleep:0.05",
        "complete:2",
        "commit",
        "close",
    ]


def test_expired_foreach_parent_terminalizes_every_reserved_child_call(
    tmp_path,
) -> None:
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'foreach-recovery.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            version = _seed_active_workflow(db)
            run = create_production_run(
                db,
                ProductionRunRequest(
                    workflow_version_id=version.id,
                    idempotency_key="foreach-parent-recovery",
                    category_key="model_3d_su",
                    queue_class="validation",
                    runtime_context={},
                    input_manifest={"content_key": "case:1"},
                    owner="platform",
                ),
                actor="admin",
            )
            claimed_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(seconds=1)
            attempt_id = claim_next_runtime_step(
                db,
                "foreach-worker",
                lease_seconds=1,
                now=claimed_at,
            )
            assert attempt_id is not None
            for key in ("item:a:attempt:1", "item:b:attempt:1"):
                db.add(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation(
                        production_run_id=run.id,
                        step_attempt_id=attempt_id,
                        invocation_key=key,
                        node_instance_id="root",
                        status="reserved",
                        provider="doubao",
                        model_id="seed",
                        evidence_json="{}",
                    )
                )
            db.flush()

            assert recover_expired_runtime_steps(
                db,
                now=claimed_at + timedelta(seconds=2),
            ) == 1

            invocations = db.scalars(
                select(WorkflowModelInvocation).order_by(
                    WorkflowModelInvocation.invocation_key
                )
            ).all()
            assert [item.status for item in invocations] == ["failed", "failed"]
            assert {
                item.error_code for item in invocations
            } == {"model_invocation_outcome_unknown"}
            assert db.get(ProductionRun, run.id).status == "failed"
    finally:
        engine.dispose()


def test_worker_process_one_prioritizes_runtime_step(monkeypatch) -> None:
    calls: list[str] = []
    event_loop_thread = threading.get_ident()

    def recover() -> int:
        assert threading.get_ident() != event_loop_thread
        calls.append("recover")
        return 0

    def process_runtime(_worker_id: str) -> bool:
        assert threading.get_ident() != event_loop_thread
        calls.append("runtime")
        return True

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "recover_runtime_once",
        recover,
        raising=False,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "process_workflow_runtime_step_once",
        process_runtime,
        raising=False,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "claim_next_job",
        lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(AssertionError("legacy job claimed")),
    )

    assert asyncio.run(worker.process_one()) is True
    assert calls == ["recover", "runtime"]


def test_evaluation_worker_group_skips_duplicate_recovery_scan(monkeypatch) -> None:
    calls: list[str] = []

    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "recover_runtime_once",
        lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(
            AssertionError("evaluation worker group must not run recovery")
        ),
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(
        worker,
        "process_workflow_runtime_step_once",
        lambda _worker_id: calls.append("runtime") or False,
    )
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "claim_next_job", lambda: None)

    assert asyncio.run(worker.process_one(run_recovery=False)) is False
    assert calls == ["runtime"]


def test_runtime_worker_reuses_persisted_global_limit(monkeypatch, tmp_path) -> None:
    fixture = _fixture_module()
    engine = create_engine(f"sqlite:///{tmp_path / 'runtime-limit.db'}")
    Base.metadata.create_all(engine)
    try:
        with Session(engine) as db:
            db.add(
                QueueSchedulerState(
                    id=1,
                    policy_version="queue-policy-v1",
                    global_limit=4,
                )
            )
            db.commit()
        captured: dict[str, int] = {}
        monkeypatch.setattr(fixture, "SessionLocal", lambda: Session(engine))
        monkeypatch.setattr(
            fixture,
            "claim_next_runtime_step",
            lambda _db, _worker_id, *, global_limit: captured.update(
                global_limit=global_limit
            ) or None,
        )

        assert fixture.process_runtime_step_once("fixture-worker") is False
        assert captured["global_limit"] == 4
    finally:
        engine.dispose()
