===== FILE frontend/src/components/personal-favorites.tsx =====
import { Star } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { Link } from "react-router-dom"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { usePreferences } from "@/lib/preferences"

export const favoriteChoices = [
  ["/workflow/dashboard", "当前待办"], ["/workflow/operations", "运行中心"],
  ["/workflow/materials/packages", "素材管理"], ["/workflow/incremental", "增量自动化"],
  ["/workflow/incremental/correction", "增量纠偏"], ["/workflow/incremental/correction-analysis", "增量纠偏分析"],
  ["/workflow/materials/jobs", "评测进度"], ["/workflow/failures", "失败处理"],
  ["/workflow/optimization/baseline-regression", "存量回归与纠偏"], ["/workflow/optimization/paired-regression", "配对回归"],
  ["/workflow/quality-assets", "质量资产"], ["/workflow/governance/workflow-manager", "工作流管理器"],
  ["/workflow/governance/model-registry", "模型登记"], ["/workflow/governance/doubao-endpoints", "豆包接入点"],
  ["/workflow/governance/model-cost", "成本对账"], ["/workflow/governance/audit", "系统审计"],
] as const

export function PersonalFavorites() {
  const preferences = usePreferences("global")
  const entries = (preferences.values.favorites ?? []).flatMap((path) => {
    const entry = favoriteChoices.find(([route]) => route === path)
    return entry ? [entry] : []
  })
  if (!entries.length) return null
  return <div className="personal-sidebar-favorites mb-3 border-b border-line pb-3"><p className="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-bold text-muted-foreground">常用入口</p>{entries.map(([path, label]) => <Link className="flex min-h-hit items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-3 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-surface-sunken" key={path} to={path}><Star size={15} />{label}</Link>)}</div>
}

export function FavoriteButton({ path }: { path: string }) {
  const preferences = usePreferences("global")
  const favorites = preferences.values.favorites ?? []
  const selected = favorites.includes(path)
  if (!favoriteChoices.some(([route]) => route === path)) return null
  return <Button data-testid="favorite-entry" variant="ghost" size="sm" className="ml-auto shrink-0" aria-pressed={selected}
    disabled={!preferences.ready || preferences.saving || (!selected && favorites.length >= 12)}
    onClick={() => preferences.save({ favorites: selected ? favorites.filter((route) => route !== path) : [...favorites, path] })}>
    <Star weight={selected ? "fill" : "regular"} />{selected ? "已收藏" : "收藏入口"}
  </Button>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/personal-mcp-settings.tsx =====
import { useState } from "react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"

type Status = { enabled: boolean; path: string; last_used_at: string | null; last_tool: string | null }
export function PersonalMcpSettings({ userId }: { userId: number }) {
  const client = useQueryClient()
  const [token, setToken] = useState("")
  const [confirmReset, setConfirmReset] = useState(false)
  const status = useQuery({ queryKey: ["personal-mcp", userId], queryFn: () => api<Status>("/api/me/mcp") })
  const url = `${window.location.origin}/api/mcp`
  const config = JSON.stringify({ mcpServers: { "label-platform": { url, headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token || "<个人令牌>"}` } } } }, null, 2)
  const rotate = useMutation({ mutationFn: () => api<{ token: string }>("/api/me/mcp/rotate", { method: "POST" }),
    onSuccess: data => { setToken(data.token); setConfirmReset(false); client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["personal-mcp"] }); toast.success("个人MCP已开通，请保存本次配置") },
    onError: error => toast.error(error.message) })
  const disable = useMutation({ mutationFn: () => api("/api/me/mcp", { method: "DELETE" }),
    onSuccess: () => { setToken(""); setConfirmReset(false); client.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["personal-mcp"] }); toast.success("MCP已停用，旧令牌已失效") }, onError: error => toast.error(error.message) })
  const download = () => {
    const blob = new Blob([config], { type: "application/json" })
    const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
    const a = document.createElement("a"); a.href = objectUrl; a.download = "label-platform-mcp.json"; a.click()
    setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl), 1000)
  }
  return <section className="employee-settings-section rounded-lg border border-line bg-white px-card py-card">
    <h2 className="visual-card-title">MCP 客户端接入</h2>
    <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">服务由中台统一运行，无需在电脑上部署服务。客户端使用你的权限访问存量工具，不能操作增量开关、生产绑定或业务投递。</p>
    {status.isLoading ? <p className="mt-5">正在读取接入状态…</p> : status.isError ? <p role="alert" className="mt-5 text-danger">{status.error.message}</p> : <>
      <dl className="mt-5 grid gap-3 text-sm"><div><dt className="font-bold">状态</dt><dd>{status.data?.enabled ? "已开通" : "未开通"}</dd></div><div><dt className="font-bold">连接地址</dt><dd className="break-all">{url}</dd></div><div><dt className="font-bold">最近成功调用</dt><dd>{status.data?.last_used_at ? `${new Date(status.data.last_used_at).toLocaleString()} · ${status.data.last_tool}` : "暂无记录"}</dd></div></dl>
      <div className="mt-5 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        {!status.data?.enabled ? <Button disabled={rotate.isPending} onClick={() => rotate.mutate()}>开通并生成配置</Button> : <><Button variant="secondary" disabled={rotate.isPending || disable.isPending} onClick={() => setConfirmReset(true)}>重置个人令牌</Button><Button variant="secondary" disabled={disable.isPending || rotate.isPending} onClick={() => disable.mutate()}>停用 MCP</Button></>}
      </div>
      {confirmReset && <div className="mt-5 rounded-lg border border-line p-4"><p>重置后，现有客户端配置立即失效，需要使用新配置重新连接。</p><div className="mt-3 flex gap-3"><Button disabled={rotate.isPending} onClick={() => rotate.mutate()}>确认重置</Button><Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => setConfirmReset(false)}>取消</Button></div></div>}
      {token && <div className="mt-6 space-y-3"><p className="text-sm">完整令牌只在本次显示，离开页面后不再回显。配置包含你的个人令牌，请自行保管。</p><textarea aria-label="MCP客户端配置" className="min-h-52 w-full rounded-lg border border-line p-3 font-mono text-sm" readOnly value={config} onFocus={event => event.currentTarget.select()} /><div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3"><Button variant="secondary" onClick={async () => { try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(config); toast.success("配置已复制") } catch { toast.error("浏览器不支持直接复制，请选中配置复制或下载") } }}>复制配置</Button><Button variant="secondary" onClick={download}>下载配置</Button><Button variant="ghost" onClick={() => setToken("")}>隐藏令牌</Button></div><p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">在支持远程 Streamable HTTP 和自定义 Authorization 请求头的客户端中添加此配置。客户端需能访问公司内网。</p></div>}
    </>}
  </section>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/projection-reconciliation-drawer.tsx =====
import { ArrowsClockwise, CheckCircle, Database, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import { toast } from "sonner"

import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { api } from "@/lib/api"
import type { ProjectionContract, ProjectionReconciliation } from "@/lib/types"

export function ProjectionReconciliationDrawer({
  contract,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
}: {
  contract: ProjectionContract | null
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const reconcile = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => api<ProjectionReconciliation>(`/api/projection-contracts/${contract?.id}/reconcile`, { method: "POST" }),
    onSuccess: async (result) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["projection-contracts"] })
      toast.success(result.status === "matched" ? "本地投影已重建并完成对账" : "本地投影存在漂移，请查看补偿信息")
    },
    onError: (error) => toast.error(error.message),
  })
  const latest = contract?.latest_reconciliation
  return (
    <SecondaryDrawer
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title={contract ? `${targetLabel(contract.target_role)} · v${contract.version}` : "投影对账"}
      description="仅操作 Label System 本地模拟表；不会写公司真实业务数据库，也不会修改 Canonical 正式标签事实。"
      footer={contract && <Button onClick={() => reconcile.mutate()} disabled={reconcile.isPending}><ArrowsClockwise />{reconcile.isPending ? "正在重建并对账" : "重建本地投影并对账"}</Button>}
    >
      {contract && <div className="space-y-6 text-sm">
        <section className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2"><Info label="目标表" value={contract.table_name} /><Info label="环境" value={contract.environment} /><Info label="模式" value={contract.mode} /><Info label="Owner" value={contract.owner} /></section>
        <section className="border-y border-[var(--line)] py-4"><div className="flex items-center gap-2"><Database /><h3 className="visual-item-title">字段映射</h3></div><div className="mt-3 divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">{Object.entries(contract.field_mappings).map(([target, source]) => <div key={target} className="grid gap-1 py-2 font-data text-xs sm:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr]"><span>{target}</span><span className="text-[var(--muted)]">← {source}</span></div>)}</div></section>
        <section><div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3"><h3 className="visual-item-title">最近对账</h3>{latest ? <Badge tone={latest.status === "matched" ? "success" : "warning"}>{latest.status === "matched" ? <><CheckCircle />一致</> : <><WarningCircle />漂移</>}</Badge> : <Badge>尚未运行</Badge>}</div>{latest ? <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2"><Info label="行数" value={String(latest.row_count)} /><Info label="缺失 / 多余" value={`${latest.missing_count} / ${latest.unexpected_count}`} /><Info label="版本一致" value={latest.version_match ? "是" : "否"} /><Info label="原因" value={latest.reason || "无"} /><Info label="实际哈希" value={latest.payload_hash.slice(0, 16)} /><Info label="预期哈希" value={latest.expected_payload_hash.slice(0, 16)} /></div> : <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">首次点击下方按钮会从正式 PublishedLabel 重建本地投影并生成行数、缺失、哈希和版本对账证据。</p>}</section>
        <section className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken p-4 text-xs leading-5 rounded-md"><p className="font-semibold">事实主权不变</p><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">投影失败只追加对账与补偿记录，不回写候选机制、人工过程或模型原始响应，也不改变任何正式标签历史。</p></section>
      </div>}
    </SecondaryDrawer>
  )
}

function targetLabel(role: ProjectionContract["target_role"]) {
  return { unified_dimension: "统一大维表", search_labels: "搜索标签小表", quality_governance: "质量治理小表" }[role]
}

function Info({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return <div className="border border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken p-3 rounded-md"><p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><p className="font-data mt-1 break-all text-xs font-semibold">{value}</p></div>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/route-error-state.tsx =====
import { ArrowClockwise, ArrowLeft, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { Component, Fragment, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from "react"
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom"

import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"

export interface RouteErrorStateProps {
  title: string
  message: string
  onRetry?: () => void
  backTo?: string
}

export interface RouteErrorBoundaryProps extends RouteErrorStateProps {
  children: ReactNode
}

interface RouteErrorBoundaryState {
  failed: boolean
  resetKey: number
}

export function RouteErrorState({ title, message, onRetry, backTo }: RouteErrorStateProps) {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-[720px] px-5 py-12 md:px-8 lg:px-10">
      <div className="rounded-lg shadow-card border border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white px-6 py-8">
        <WarningCircle className="text-warning" size={32} weight="fill" aria-hidden="true" />
        <h1 className="visual-page-title mt-4">{title}</h1>
        <p className="mt-3 text-sm leading-6 text-[var(--muted)]">{message}</p>
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {onRetry && <Button onClick={onRetry}><ArrowClockwise />重新加载</Button>}
          {backTo && <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => navigate(backTo)}><ArrowLeft />返回</Button>}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export class RouteErrorBoundary extends Component<RouteErrorBoundaryProps, RouteErrorBoundaryState> {
  state: RouteErrorBoundaryState = { failed: false, resetKey: 0 }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(): Partial<RouteErrorBoundaryState> {
    return { failed: true }
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error("Route render failed", error, info.componentStack)
  }

  private retry = () => {
    this.props.onRetry?.()
    this.setState(({ resetKey }) => ({ failed: false, resetKey: resetKey + 1 }))
  }

  render() {
    const { children, title, message, backTo } = this.props
    if (this.state.failed) {
      return <RouteErrorState title={title} message={message} onRetry={this.retry} backTo={backTo} />
    }
    return <Fragment key={this.state.resetKey}>{children}</Fragment>
  }
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/runtime-run-drawer.tsx =====
import { ArrowClockwise, Pause, Play, Stop, WarningCircle } from "@phosphor-icons/react"
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"

import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import { runtimeApi } from "@/lib/runtime-api"
import type { ProductionRunSummary, RuntimeAction, RuntimeTimelineItem } from "@/lib/types"
import { runtimeNodeDetails, type RuntimeNodeDetail } from "@/features/workflow-manager/runtime-node-details"
import { useAccess } from "@/lib/access"
import { runtimeStatusLabel } from "@/features/workflow-manager/operator-presentation"
import { formatPlatformTime } from "../lib/platform-time"

const actionLabels: Record<RuntimeAction, string> = {
  pause: "暂停",
  resume: "恢复",
  retry: "重试",
  cancel: "取消",
}

const actionIcons: Record<RuntimeAction, typeof Pause> = {
  pause: Pause,
  resume: Play,
  retry: ArrowClockwise,
  cancel: Stop,
}

function statusTone(status: ProductionRunSummary["status"]) {
  if (status === "succeeded") return "success" as const
  if (status === "failed" || status === "blocked") return "danger" as const
  if (status === "retryable" || status === "paused" || status === "waiting_external") return "warning" as const
  return "neutral" as const
}

export function RuntimeRunDrawer({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  run,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  run: ProductionRunSummary | null
}) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const access = useAccess()
  const canAct = (name: RuntimeAction) => name === "retry" ? access.can("production:rerun", run?.category_key) : !!access.user?.is_admin
  const runKey = run?.run_key ?? ""
  const detail = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["runtime-run", runKey],
    queryFn: () => runtimeApi.getRun(runKey),
    enabled: open && Boolean(runKey),
    refetchInterval: open ? 2500 : false,
  })
  const timeline = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["runtime-timeline", runKey],
    queryFn: () => runtimeApi.getTimeline(runKey),
    enabled: open && Boolean(runKey),
    refetchInterval: open ? 2500 : false,
  })
  const snapshot = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["runtime-snapshot", runKey],
    queryFn: () => runtimeApi.getSnapshot(runKey),
    enabled: open && Boolean(runKey),
  })
  const action = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (name: RuntimeAction) => canAct(name) ? runtimeApi.action(runKey, name) : Promise.reject(new Error(access.readOnlyReason)),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["runtime-runs"] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["runtime-run", runKey] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["runtime-timeline", runKey] }),
      ])
    },
  })
  const current = detail.data ?? run
  const frozenNodes = runtimeNodeDetails(snapshot.data?.snapshot)
  const frozenNodeById = new Map(frozenNodes.map((node) => [node.nodeInstanceId, node]))

  return (
    <SecondaryDrawer
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      title="运行详情"
      description="查看冻结版本、步骤尝试、检查点和可执行恢复动作。"
      size="wide"
      footer={current && current.allowed_actions.length ? (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
          {current.allowed_actions.map((name) => {
            const Icon = actionIcons[name]
            return (
              <Button
                key={name}
                variant={name === "cancel" ? "secondary" : "primary"}
                disabled={!canAct(name) || action.isPending}
                onClick={() => action.mutate(name)}
              >
                <Icon size={17} />{actionLabels[name]}
              </Button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      ) : undefined}
    >
      {!current ? (
        <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">未选择运行。</p>
      ) : (
        <div className="space-y-6">
          <section className="grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
            <Evidence label="状态"><Badge tone={statusTone(current.status)}>{runtimeStatusLabel(current.status)}</Badge></Evidence>
            <Evidence label="工作流版本">{current.workflow_version}</Evidence>
            <Evidence label="当前步骤">{current.current_step_key ?? "已结束"}</Evidence>
            <Evidence label="最后检查点">{current.last_checkpoint_id ? "#" + current.last_checkpoint_id : "暂无"}</Evidence>
          </section>

          {current.external_wait && (
            <ExternalWaitEvidence
              wait={current.external_wait}
              nodeById={frozenNodeById}
            />
          )}

          {(current.error_message || current.blockers.length > 0) && (
            <section className="border-y border-danger bg-danger-wash px-4 py-4">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold"><WarningCircle />阻塞与错误</div>
              {current.error_message && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-danger">{current.error_code}: {current.error_message}</p>}
              {current.blockers.map((blocker, index) => (
                <p key={index} className="mt-2 text-xs text-danger">
                  {typeof blocker === "string" ? blocker : blocker.message ?? blocker.code ?? "未知阻塞"}
                </p>
              ))}
            </section>
          )}

          {frozenNodes.length > 0 && (
            <section>
              <h3 className="visual-item-title">本轮评测机制</h3>
              <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">按本次运行冻结的工作流展示，后续编辑不会改变这里的历史证据。</p>
              <div className="mt-3 grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2">
                {frozenNodes.map((node, index) => (
                  <article key={node.nodeInstanceId} className="border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-white px-4 py-3">
                    <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">步骤 {index + 1} · {node.kindLabel}</p>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold">{node.displayName}</p>
                    <RuntimeNodeFacts node={node} />
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}

          <section>
            <h3 className="visual-item-title">步骤时间线</h3>
            <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">
              {(timeline.data?.items ?? []).map((item) => {
                const node = frozenNodeById.get(item.step_key)
                return <article key={item.id} className="border-y border-[var(--line)] px-4 py-4">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">步骤 {item.sequence + 1} · 第 {item.attempt_no} 次尝试</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-sm font-bold">{node?.displayName ?? item.step_key}</p>
                      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{node?.kindLabel ?? item.step_type} · 节点标识 {item.step_key}</p>
                    </div>
                    <Badge tone={item.status === "succeeded" ? "success" : item.status === "failed" ? "danger" : "neutral"}>{runtimeStatusLabel(item.status)}</Badge>
                  </div>
                  <dl className="mt-3 grid gap-2 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2">
                    <Evidence label="脚本版本">{item.script_version}</Evidence>
                    <Evidence label="队列">{item.queue_class ?? "未入队"}</Evidence>
                    <Evidence label="输入哈希">{item.input_hash}</Evidence>
                    <Evidence label="输出哈希">{item.output_hash ?? "暂无"}</Evidence>
                    <Evidence label="checkpoint_hash">{item.checkpoint_hash ?? "暂无"}</Evidence>
                    <Evidence label="租约责任">{item.lease_owner ?? "无"}</Evidence>
                  </dl>
                  {node && <RuntimeNodeFacts node={node} />}
                  <ConnectorEvidence evidence={item.connector_evidence} />
                  {item.last_error_message && <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-danger">{item.last_error_code}: {item.last_error_message}</p>}
                </article>
              })}
              {timeline.isLoading && <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取步骤证据…</p>}
              {!timeline.isLoading && !timeline.data?.items.length && <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">暂无步骤尝试。</p>}
            </div>
          </section>

          <section>
            <h3 className="visual-item-title">冻结快照</h3>
            <p className="mt-2 visual-code text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">SHA-256 {current.snapshot_hash}</p>
            {snapshot.data ? (
              <div className="mt-3 grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
                <Evidence label="快照格式">{snapshot.data.snapshot.schema_version || "未标记"}</Evidence>
                <Evidence label="冻结步骤">{frozenNodes.length} 个</Evidence>
                <Evidence label="冻结脚本">{snapshot.data.snapshot.scripts.length} 个</Evidence>
                <Evidence label="历史可追溯">哈希已校验</Evidence>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">正在读取冻结快照…</p>
            )}
            <p className="mt-3 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">敏感正文与供应商原始响应不在运营摘要中展示；节点版本、模型参数和执行证据已在上方逐项列出。</p>
          </section>

          {action.isError && <p className="text-sm text-danger">动作执行失败，请刷新后重试。</p>}
        </div>
      )}
    </SecondaryDrawer>
  )
}

function ConnectorEvidence({ evidence }: { evidence: RuntimeTimelineItem["connector_evidence"] }) {
  if (!evidence) return null
  const copyByMode: Record<
    NonNullable<RuntimeTimelineItem["connector_evidence"]>["io_mode"],
    [string, string]
  > = {
    imported_sample: ["本轮使用导入素材", "未读取外部上游"],
    evidence_only: ["本轮仅保留写入证据", "未写入外部下游"],
    intake_event: ["已使用接收成功的上游数据", "本步骤不重复访问上游系统"],
    transactional_outbox: ["结果已进入安全待写队列", "由投递任务完成写入和回读核对"],
    workflow_input: ["使用本轮工作流输入", "未访问外部上游"],
    projection_only: ["已生成下游结果格式", "未执行外部写入"],
    unavailable: ["本轮未联网检测", "没有导入可回放证据，因此明确标记为未检测"],
    imported_evidence: ["已回放导入证据", "存量回归没有访问外部图片查询服务"],
    callback_fact: ["已收到图片查询结果", "结果来自已通过合同和摘要校验的供应商回传"],
  }
  const copy = copyByMode[evidence.io_mode]
  const identifiers = [
    evidence.contract_version != null ? `接口版本 V${evidence.contract_version}` : null,
    evidence.outbox_event_id != null ? `待写记录 #${evidence.outbox_event_id}` : null,
    evidence.payload_hash ? `内容校验 ${evidence.payload_hash.slice(0, 12)}` : null,
  ].filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value))
  return (
    <div className="mt-3 border-l-4 border-primary bg-active-surface px-3 py-2">
      <p className="text-xs font-bold">{copy[0]}</p>
      <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{copy[1]}。</p>
      {evidence.first_seen && (
        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-2">
          <Evidence label="供应商最早发现时间">
            {evidence.first_seen.supplier_first_seen_at
              ? operatorDate(evidence.first_seen.supplier_first_seen_at)
              : "供应商未返回发现时间"}
          </Evidence>
          <Evidence label="查询结论">
            {firstSeenStatusLabel(evidence.first_seen.status)}
          </Evidence>
          <Evidence label="证据来源">{evidence.first_seen.provider}</Evidence>
          <Evidence label="可信度">{Math.round(evidence.first_seen.confidence * 100)}%</Evidence>
        </div>
      )}
      {identifiers.length > 0 && <p className="mt-1 font-data text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{identifiers.join(" · ")}</p>}
    </div>
  )
}

function ExternalWaitEvidence({
  wait,
  nodeById,
}: {
  wait: NonNullable<ProductionRunSummary["external_wait"]>
  nodeById: Map<string, RuntimeNodeDetail>
}) {
  const requestNode = nodeById.get(wait.request_node_instance_id)
  const resumeNode = nodeById.get(wait.resume_node_instance_id)
  return (
    <section className="border-y border-warning bg-alias-surface-078 px-4 py-4">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h3 className="visual-item-title">等待外部系统</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">
            {requestNode?.displayName ?? wait.request_node_instance_id}
            {" → "}
            {resumeNode?.displayName ?? wait.resume_node_instance_id}
          </p>
        </div>
        <Badge tone={wait.state === "waiting" ? "warning" : wait.state === "resumed" ? "success" : "danger"}>
          {runtimeStatusLabel(wait.state)}
        </Badge>
      </div>
      <dl className="mt-3 grid gap-px bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-3">
        <Evidence label="等待开始">{operatorDate(wait.created_at)}</Evidence>
        <Evidence label="等待截止">{operatorDate(wait.deadline_at)}</Evidence>
        <Evidence label="恢复时间">
          {wait.resumed_at ? operatorDate(wait.resumed_at) : "尚未收到回传"}
        </Evidence>
      </dl>
      <details className="visual-native-details mt-3 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">
        <summary className="cursor-pointer font-semibold text-foreground">查看技术关联信息</summary>
        <p className="mt-2 break-all font-data leading-5">
          关联号 {wait.correlation_id} · 回传合同 #{wait.expected_contract_version_id}
          {wait.callback_intake_event_id ? ` · 回传事实 #${wait.callback_intake_event_id}` : ""}
        </p>
      </details>
    </section>
  )
}

function operatorDate(value: string): string {
  return formatPlatformTime(value, "时间待确认")
}

function firstSeenStatusLabel(
  status: NonNullable<
    NonNullable<RuntimeTimelineItem["connector_evidence"]>["first_seen"]
  >["status"],
): string {
  return {
    found: "已找到公开网络记录",
    not_found: "供应商未找到记录",
    unavailable: "本轮未联网检测",
    failed: "查询没有成功",
  }[status] ?? "结论待确认"
}

function RuntimeNodeFacts({ node }: { node: RuntimeNodeDetail }) {
  const facts = [
    node.moduleTypeLabel ? `模块：${node.moduleTypeLabel}` : null,
    node.componentVersion ? `版本：${node.componentVersion}` : null,
    node.modelName ? `模型：${node.modelName}` : null,
    node.maxTokens != null ? `最大 Token：${node.maxTokens}` : null,
    node.thinkingLabel ? `思考层级：${node.thinkingLabel}` : null,
  ].filter((value): value is string => Boolean(value))
  if (!facts.length) return null
  return <p className="mt-2 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">{facts.join(" · ")}</p>
}

function Evidence({ label, children }: { label: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="min-w-0 bg-white px-3 py-3">
      <dt className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</dt>
      <dd className="mt-1 break-all font-data text-xs">{children}</dd>
    </div>
  )
}

import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import type { BaselineSemanticQualityMetrics } from "@/lib/types"

export function SemanticQualityDrawer({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  data,
  loading,
  error,
}: {
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
  data?: BaselineSemanticQualityMetrics
  loading: boolean
  error: Error | null
}) {
  return <SecondaryDrawer open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange} size="wide" title="语义字段质量" description="按实体 ID 统计 Precision、Recall、映射覆盖、冲突和人工纠偏证据；不替代原有 L1–L5 等级矩阵。">
    {loading ? <div className="h-52 animate-pulse bg-surface-sunken" /> : error ? <div className="border border-danger bg-danger-wash px-4 py-4 text-sm text-danger rounded-md">语义字段质量加载失败：{error.message}</div> : !data ? <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">当前轮次尚未形成语义字段质量证据。</p> : <div className="space-y-6">
      {data.evidence?.status === "unavailable_historical" && <div className="border-y border-warning bg-warning-wash px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5 text-warning">该历史轮次未冻结语义黄金真值，不使用当前黄金集回算，避免历史指标漂移。请用新版回归任务生成可追溯指标。</div>}
      <section className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-px border-y border-[var(--line)] bg-[var(--line)] sm:grid-cols-4"><Metric label="宏 Precision" value={formatRate(data.aggregates.macro_precision)} /><Metric label="宏 Recall" value={formatRate(data.aggregates.macro_recall)} /><Metric label="微 Precision" value={formatRate(data.aggregates.micro_precision)} /><Metric label="微 Recall" value={formatRate(data.aggregates.micro_recall)} /></section>
      <section className="overflow-x-auto border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
        <table className="w-full min-w-[1120px] border-collapse text-left text-xs">
          <thead><tr className="border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken"><th className="px-3 py-3">字段</th><th className="px-3 py-3">Precision</th><th className="px-3 py-3">Recall</th><th className="px-3 py-3">映射覆盖</th><th className="px-3 py-3">未映射率</th><th className="px-3 py-3">冲突率</th><th className="px-3 py-3">空值语义</th><th className="px-3 py-3">纠偏率</th><th className="px-3 py-3">审核覆盖</th></tr></thead>
          <tbody>{Object.values(data.fields).map((field) => <tr key={field.field_key} className="border-b border-[var(--line)] last:border-0"><td className="visual-code px-3 py-3 font-semibold">{field.field_key}</td><td className="font-data px-3 py-3">{formatRate(field.precision)}</td><td className="font-data px-3 py-3">{formatRate(field.recall)}</td><td className="font-data px-3 py-3">{formatRate(field.mapping_coverage)}</td><td className="font-data px-3 py-3">{formatRate(field.unmapped_rate)}</td><td className="font-data px-3 py-3">{formatRate(field.conflict_rate)}</td><td className="font-data px-3 py-3">{formatRate(field.null_semantics_accuracy)}</td><td className="font-data px-3 py-3">{formatRate(field.correction_rate)}</td><td className="font-data px-3 py-3">{formatRate(field.review_coverage)}</td></tr>)}</tbody>
        </table>
      </section>
      <section className="border-y border-[var(--line-strong)] bg-white">
        <div className="border-b border-[var(--line)] bg-surface-sunken px-4 py-3">
          <h3 className="visual-item-title">待字典审核</h3>
          <p className="mt-1 text-xs leading-5 text-[var(--muted)]">只展示未命中批准字典的原词和近似建议；建议不会自动进入正式标签，也不阻塞本轮评测。</p>
        </div>
        {(data.evidence?.unmapped_candidates ?? []).length ? <div className="divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
          {(data.evidence?.unmapped_candidates ?? []).map((candidate, index) => <div key={`${candidate.asset_id}-${candidate.field_key}-${candidate.raw_value}-${index}`} className="grid gap-2 px-4 py-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[9rem_1fr_2fr]">
            <p className="visual-code font-semibold">{candidate.field_key}</p>
            <p>{candidate.raw_value}</p>
            <p className="text-[var(--muted)]">{candidate.suggestions.length ? candidate.suggestions.map((suggestion) => `${suggestion.canonical_value}（${(suggestion.score * 100).toFixed(0)}%）`).join("、") : "暂无近似建议"}</p>
          </div>)}
        </div> : <p className="px-4 py-4 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">本轮没有待字典审核词。</p>}
      </section>
      <p className="border-l-2 border-primary bg-active-surface px-4 py-3 text-xs leading-5">零分母显示“—”。该证据与精确等级准确率、相邻等级准确率、推荐档、常规档、过滤档及完整五档矩阵并行展示。</p>
    </div>}
  </SecondaryDrawer>
}

function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) { return <div className="bg-white p-4"><p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><p className="font-data mt-2 text-xl font-bold">{value}</p></div> }
function formatRate(value: number | null | undefined) { return value == null ? "—" : `${(value * 100).toFixed(1)}%` }

===== FILE frontend/src/components/table-preferences.tsx =====
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button"
import type { TablePreference } from "@/lib/types"

export type PersonalColumn = { key: string; label: string; width: number; fixed?: boolean }

export function orderedColumns(columns: PersonalColumn[], settings: TablePreference) {
  const saved = new Map(settings.columns?.map((column, index) => [column.key, { ...column, index }]))
  const resolved = columns.map((column, index) => ({ ...column, visible: column.fixed || (saved.get(column.key)?.visible ?? true),
    width: saved.get(column.key)?.width ?? column.width,
    order: saved.get(column.key)?.index ?? 1000 + index,
  }))
  const movable = resolved.filter((column) => !column.fixed).sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order)
  let next = 0
  return resolved.map((column) => column.fixed ? column : movable[next++])
}

export function TablePreferences({ columns, value, onChange, disabled = false }: {
  columns: PersonalColumn[]; value: TablePreference; onChange: (next: Partial<TablePreference>) => void; disabled?: boolean
}) {
  const ordered = orderedColumns(columns, value)
  const save = (next: typeof ordered) => onChange({ columns: next.map(({ key, width, visible }) => ({ key, width, visible })) })
  return <details className="personal-table-preferences visual-native-details max-w-full rounded-[8px] border border-[var(--line)] bg-white p-3">
    <summary className="cursor-pointer text-sm font-semibold">表格设置</summary>
    <fieldset disabled={disabled} className="mt-3 min-w-0 space-y-3">
      <label className="flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">行间距
        <select className="visual-native-select" value={value.density ?? "comfortable"} onChange={(event) => onChange({ density: event.target.value as TablePreference["density"] })}>
          <option value="comfortable">舒适</option><option value="compact">紧凑</option>
        </select>
      </label>
      <p className="text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">列顺序、显示和宽度随账号保存。</p>
      <div className="personal-table-columns max-h-72 overflow-y-auto divide-y divide-[var(--line)]">
        {ordered.map((column, index) => <div key={column.key} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 py-2">
          <label className="visual-native-choice flex min-w-44 flex-1 items-center gap-2 text-xs"><input type="checkbox" className="visual-native-checkbox" checked={column.visible} disabled={column.fixed}
            onChange={(event) => save(ordered.map((item) => item.key === column.key ? { ...item, visible: event.target.checked } : item))} />{column.label}</label>
          <label className="text-xs">宽度 <input type="number" aria-label={`${column.label}列宽`} className="visual-native-input w-20" min={80} max={600} step={20} value={column.width}
            onChange={(event) => { const width = Number(event.target.value); if (Number.isInteger(width) && width >= 80 && width <= 600) save(ordered.map((item) => item.key === column.key ? { ...item, width } : item)) }} /></label>
          <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" aria-label={`${column.label}前移`} disabled={column.fixed || index === 0 || ordered[index - 1].fixed} onClick={() => { const next = [...ordered]; [next[index - 1], next[index]] = [next[index], next[index - 1]]; save(next) }}>前移</Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" aria-label={`${column.label}后移`} disabled={column.fixed || index === ordered.length - 1 || ordered[index + 1].fixed} onClick={() => { const next = [...ordered]; [next[index + 1], next[index]] = [next[index], next[index + 1]]; save(next) }}>后移</Button>
        </div>)}
      </div>
      <Button size="sm" variant="secondary" onClick={() => onChange({ columns: [], density: "comfortable" })}>恢复默认表格</Button>
    </fieldset>
  </details>
}

===== FILE frontend/src/components/tag-demand-contract-drawer.tsx =====
import { SecondaryDrawer } from "@/components/workspace-page"
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge"
import type { TagDemandContract } from "@/lib/types"
import type { ReactNode } from "react"
import { formatPlatformTime } from "../lib/platform-time"

const statusLabels: Record<TagDemandContract["status"], string> = {
  draft: "草稿",
  candidate: "候选",
  active: "现役",
  retired: "已退役",
}

export function TagDemandContractDrawer({
  contract,
  open,
  onOpenChange,
}: {
  contract: TagDemandContract | null
  open: boolean
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void
}) {
  return (
    <SecondaryDrawer
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={onOpenChange}
      size="wide"
      title={contract ? `${contract.contract_key} · v${contract.version}` : "字段需求合同详情"}
      description="字段矩阵、空值语义、执行变体和投影映射只在二级抽屉查看，避免一级页面平铺。"
    >
      {!contract ? <p className="text-sm text-[var(--muted)]">请选择一个合同版本。</p> : <div className="space-y-7">
        <section className="grid gap-4 border-y border-[var(--line)] py-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <Detail label="状态"><Badge tone={contract.status === "active" ? "success" : contract.status === "candidate" ? "warning" : "neutral"}>{statusLabels[contract.status]}</Badge></Detail>
          <Detail label="合同哈希"><span className="visual-code break-all text-xs">{contract.contract_hash}</span></Detail>
          <Detail label="创建信息"><span>{contract.created_by} · {formatPlatformTime(contract.created_at)}</span></Detail>
        </section>

        <section>
          <SectionTitle>平台字段</SectionTitle>
          <div className="mt-3 overflow-hidden border-y border-[var(--line)]">
            <div className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_110px_90px_110px] gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]"><span>字段</span><span>基数</span><span>本地化</span><span>最大值</span></div>
            {Object.values(contract.definition.semantic_schema.fields).map((field) => <div key={field.field_key} className="grid grid-cols-[minmax(0,1fr)_110px_90px_110px] gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3 text-sm last:border-0"><span className="font-bold">{field.field_key}<small className="ml-2 text-xs font-normal text-[var(--muted)]">{field.vocabulary_owner}</small></span><span>{field.cardinality}</span><span>{field.localized ? "是" : "否"}</span><span>{field.max_values}</span></div>)}
          </div>
        </section>

        <section>
          <SectionTitle>类目适用性</SectionTitle>
          <div className="mt-3 space-y-3">{Object.entries(contract.definition.category_applicability).map(([category, fields]) => <div key={category} className="border-y border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3"><div className="font-bold">{category}</div><div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">{Object.entries(fields).map(([field, status]) => <span key={field} className="border border-[var(--line)] px-2 py-1 text-xs rounded-md"><strong>{field}</strong> · {status}</span>)}</div></div>)}</div>
        </section>

        <section>
          <SectionTitle>源身份合同</SectionTitle>
          {contract.definition.source_identity ? (
            <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-4 text-xs sm:grid-cols-2">
              <Detail label="来源 / 对象粒度"><span>{contract.definition.source_identity.source_system} · {contract.definition.source_identity.object_grain}</span></Detail>
              <Detail label="候选复合键"><span className="visual-code">{contract.definition.source_identity.identity_fields.join(" + ")}</span></Detail>
              <Detail label="可选消歧字段"><span>{contract.definition.source_identity.optional_disambiguator ?? "无"}</span></Detail>
              <Detail label="版本 / 删除字段"><span>{contract.definition.source_identity.version_field} · {contract.definition.source_identity.deletion_field}</span></Detail>
              <Detail label="唯一性状态"><Badge tone={contract.definition.source_identity.uniqueness_status === "verified" ? "success" : contract.definition.source_identity.uniqueness_status === "conflict" ? "danger" : "warning"}>{contract.definition.source_identity.uniqueness_status}</Badge></Detail>
              <Detail label="签认证据哈希"><span className="visual-code break-all">{contract.definition.source_identity.verification_evidence_hash ?? "尚未绑定"}</span></Detail>
            </div>
          ) : <p className="mt-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-4 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">v1 合同沿用旧身份口径；升级为 v2 后才可绑定源身份签认证据。</p>}
        </section>

        <section>
          <SectionTitle>字段供给路径</SectionTitle>
          {Object.keys(contract.definition.field_supply ?? {}).length ? (
            <div className="mt-3 overflow-x-auto border-y border-[var(--line)]">
              <div className="min-w-[920px]">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-[150px_100px_120px_170px_150px_80px_1fr] gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-2 text-xs font-semibold text-[var(--muted)]"><span>字段</span><span>命名空间</span><span>生产方式</span><span>Authority</span><span>Owner</span><span>SLA</span><span>空值 / 回退</span></div>
                {Object.entries(contract.definition.field_supply ?? {}).map(([fieldKey, supply]) => <div key={fieldKey} className="grid grid-cols-[150px_100px_120px_170px_150px_80px_1fr] gap-3 border-b border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3 text-xs last:border-0"><strong>{fieldKey}</strong><span>{supply.fact_namespace}</span><span>{supply.production_method}</span><span>{supply.source_authority}</span><span>{supply.owner}</span><span>{supply.freshness_sla_hours}h</span><span>{supply.null_semantics.join(" / ")} · {supply.rollback_strategy}</span></div>)}
              </div>
            </div>
          ) : <p className="mt-3 border-y border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-4 text-xs text-[var(--muted)]">当前 v1 合同没有版本化字段供给路径。</p>}
        </section>

        <section>
          <SectionTitle>执行变体</SectionTitle>
          <div className="mt-3 space-y-2">{contract.definition.execution_variants.map((variant) => <div key={`${variant.category_key}-${variant.site_scope}-${variant.asset_scope}-${variant.prompt_version}`} className="grid gap-2 border-y border-[var(--line)] px-3 py-3 text-xs sm:grid-cols-[1fr_1fr_1fr_1fr]"><span>{variant.category_key}</span><span>{variant.site_scope} · {variant.locale}</span><span>{variant.asset_scope} · {variant.prompt_variant}</span><span className="visual-code">{variant.prompt_version} / {variant.model_version}</span>{Object.keys(variant.field_applicability_overrides ?? {}).length > 0 && <div className="sm:col-span-4 border-t border-[var(--line)] pt-2"><strong>执行变体覆盖：</strong>{Object.entries(variant.field_applicability_overrides ?? {}).map(([field, status]) => <span key={field} className="ml-2">{variant.asset_scope} → {field}: {status}</span>)}</div>}</div>)}</div>
        </section>

        <section>
          <SectionTitle>质量门槛与投影</SectionTitle>
          <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">{Object.entries(contract.definition.quality_gates).map(([field, gate]) => <div key={field} className="border border-[var(--line)] p-3 text-xs rounded-md"><div className="font-bold">{field}</div><p className="mt-2 text-[var(--muted)]">Precision ≥ {gate.min_precision} · Recall ≥ {gate.min_recall}</p><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">映射覆盖 ≥ {gate.min_mapping_coverage} · 冲突率 ≤ {gate.max_conflict_rate}</p></div>)}{contract.definition.projection_targets.map((target) => <div key={target.target_key} className="border border-[var(--line)] p-3 text-xs rounded-md"><div className="font-bold">{target.target_key}</div><p className="mt-1 text-[var(--muted)]">{target.locale} · {target.mode}</p></div>)}</div>
        </section>
      </div>}
    </SecondaryDrawer>
  )
}

function SectionTitle({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) { return <h2 className="visual-card-title">{children}</h2> }
function Detail({ label, children }: { label: string; children: ReactNode }) { return <div className="space-y-1 text-xs"><p className="text-[var(--muted)]">{label}</p><div>{children}</div></div> }
